Namgung Do-Wei bit his lips at the fierce shout. The hand pushing his shoulder turned more violent, and naturally, Namgung Do-Wei couldn’t help but run faster.

“Listen carefully!”

“... uh?”

“What is needed for someone who can stand in front of others is not skill.”

Chung Myung’s voice pierced his ears clearly.

“It is qualification!”

“....”

Chung Myung looked at the Namgung family with cold eyes. The Namgung family deserved to be saved, and Namgung Do-Wei deserved to stand at the front.

Then, what he must do was very clear.

“Then you should be opening the way!”

At that moment, a reassuring voice that would never be heard in the world came.

“I will protect your back!”

The dagger of Tang Gunak flew past Namgung Do-Wei’s head and sank into the chest of the pirate in front.

“Go!”

Namgung Do-Wei turned around at Chung Myung’s shout and kicked the ground as he charged ahead.

It was odd.

The moment he charged ahead, each and every one of those he passed by sent him a strong look.

As if they were waiting for the moment.

And those eyes were enough to make Namgung Do-Wei, who was groaning from his injuries, use every last bit of his strength.

“Ahhhhh!”

White sword qi surged from the tip of Namgung Do-Wei’s sword as he passed through Baek Cheon and charged ahead.

Kwaaang!

The Emperor’s Sword Form!

The Emperor’s Sword was designed to move forward relentlessly. His sword was meant to destroy the pirates and protect those who followed him!

The deep insight that Namgung Hwang realized at the end was passed onto his son. The next generation will carry on what the previous generation achieved. Isn’t that what a family exists for?

“Get out of my way!”

Kwaaang!

White Sword qi shot out from the sword as it swung vertically. The pirates were swept away without even being able to resist.

“Young Lordddd!”

A strained voice burst out from Namgung Myung.

The sight of a sword that only the direct line of Namgung’s lord could master held immense meaning for them.

He couldn’t yet compare this to Namgung Hwang, but it was definitely the Emperor’s Sword Form! It was the pure white qi that symbolized the Namgung family.

“Cough!”

Namgung Do-Wei’s body lurched forward for a moment.

Because he had forcibly raised his qi, his vision was turning dizzy, and his legs trembled as if he would collapse at any moment.

“D-Die!”

A pirate swung their blade at him, taking advantage of the gap. Showing weakness on the battlefield meant immediate death!

But...

“How dare you!”

At that moment, a black shadow rose above Namgung Do-Wei’s head.

“M-Mount Hua’s Sword Saint!”

The faces of those who recognized him turned pale.

Paaat!

Dozens of red sword qi forms poured out from Chung Myung’s sword. They accurately pierced the necks of those who rushed at Namgung Do-Wei.

The pirates couldn’t even scream and fell with the force they had run toward him.

Chung Myung, who landed right next to Namgung Do-Wei, grabbed his shoulder and pulled him up. However, before he could offer help, Namgung Do-Wei raised his head and steadied himself.

Kwang!

With the sound of feet stomping down, Namgung Do-Wei’s sword once again emitted a white flash.

Those who were waiting for him were right there.

So, at least...

‘I cannot speak of this as being hard!’

Kwaaang!

Namgung Do-Wei, who unleashed the sword once more, staggered. He stabbed the sword into the neck of the approaching pirate, barely managing to get up from the ground and somehow moving forward.

Thud!

He could never take this step alone. He would never have been able to stand up to those who tried to stop him.

But.

At least, at this moment, he wasn’t alone. He could feel it. Someone was guarding his back. Someone was waiting for him to move ahead!

‘Move!’

Blood flowed down his nose and mouth, but Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes held firm determination.

“I!”

A blood-boiling scream came out of his mouth.

“I am the Lord of the Namgung family!”

Namgung Do-Wei’s sword, which rushed toward the pirates blocking his way, created a flickering halo of light.

The sword qi glowed faintly as if it would go out at any moment.

But the sword was never meant to clash with the pirates’ harpoons.

Kaaakang!

The sword qi, which was supposed to cut down and destroy the pirates, was hit by a harpoon and bounced off weakly.

“Ah...”

A pitiful sigh escaped Namgung Do-Wei’s mouth. But right then.

“You did good, kid.”

An explosive sword qi poured out from behind his back. The red sword qi shot ahead in all directions in an instant and soon drew out vivid red plum blossoms.

The plum blossoms began to sway like a storm and sweep away the pirates in front. The screams of life and death echoed through the air.

And Namgung Do-Wei saw.

A clear path opened up between him and the pirates, blocking the Namgung family.

There was no need to think any further.

“Run!”

Namgung Do-Wei gritted his teeth and kicked the ground.

Towards that path, the path he must take!

Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes, tracing the path he had carved with blood, met those of someone watching him.

“Young lordddd!”

At Namgung Myung’s tearful shout, Namgung Do-Wei collapsed into his arms with a pure smile.

Clench!

Namgung Myung used all his strength to embrace Namgung Do-Wei.

“You did good, really... really good. Young lord... no.”

He bit his lip tightly and corrected himself.

“Lord.”

As if the emotional reunion were happening to someone else, Chung Myung passed by the two, striking down one of the pirates in a single breath. Then he shouted.

“Kill them all!”

“Woahhhh!”

Finally, the Mount Hua swordsmen reached the Namgung family and began to sweep away the pirates with great force.

Chapter 972: Know That This Is Glory, Kid. (2)

“E-Eik! Diee!”

A harpoon flew swiftly towards Namgung Pyung’s neck.

Namgung Pyung instinctively realized he couldn’t stop it and tightly shut his eyes. But at that moment—

Kakang!

A sword, appearing like a ray of light from nowhere, violently struck the harpoon aimed at Namgung Pyung’s neck. The sword, maintaining its force, pierced the neck of the harpoon-wielding pirate.

Puaaah!

Namgung Pyung blankly stared at the blood gushing from the harpoon holder’s neck.

“Back.”

“....”

“Back.”

’Uh? Ah... yes!“

Namgung Pyung answered in surprise. The female swordsman, who glanced at him, stood in front of Namgung Pyung without a sword.

That was the start.

“OHHHHH!”

It was like a landslide.

Like a fierce landslide, the black waves surged along the path between the pirates.

The white swords, contrasting with the black robes, cut through the air like the wind.

It was a spectacle akin to a disaster for the pirates who were chasing after the Namgung family.

Everything in the world was relative.

From the Namgung family’s point of view, exhausted beyond belief, the pirates attacking them seemed no different from beasts. Yet, in front of Mount Hua, who were advancing with unstoppable momentum, they were nothing more than leaves being swept away.

Paaat!

The Mount Hua swordsmen who had come ahead swung their swords all at once. As if one person was wielding them, a dozen or so sword forms traced the same path, coldly piercing the hearts of the pirates who were unable to comprehend what was happening.

“Accck!”

“F-Fucking dog... ugh....”

The eyes of the pirates, who saw their comrades become souls in an instant, were filled with fear.

Those who pursue profit over purpose become more cruel and violent than anyone else when they have the upper hand, but they become more cowardly than anyone else when their lives are in danger.

It was only natural. Why should those who value their lives the most endure a battlefield where they will surely die?

Unfortunately for them, there was someone here who understood the nature of the Evil Sects better than anyone else.

Kwaang!

Someone stepped on the ground and shouted.

“Break it!”

As soon as the words were out, five black shadows rose into the air.

Baek Cheon, Yu Yiseol, Yoon Jong, Hae Yeon, and even Jo Gul, who had caught up with the pirates, rushed with all their might.

They carried out his will as if they were the swords wielded by Chung Myung.

“Strike!”

As soon as Baek Cheon’s command was given, red plum blossoms bloomed from the four swords simultaneously.

These were not the swords they had seen so far but the most magnificent forms of them.

As if they were going to cover Plum Blossom Island by painting it with plum blossoms, countless plum blossoms bloomed from the four swords in an eerie manner.

And in the center.

“Ah-mi-ta-ba!”

A golden qi burst out explosively, along with the majestic shout of Buddha.

Buddha’s Glory Illuminates the Universe!

The sight of the magnificent Buddhist light rising between the red plum blossoms surrounding the Namgung family was truly overwhelming.

Flutter!

The plum blossom leaves, which flew up like fluttering petals, rained down on the rebels. And the golden light crashed down on the pirates.

“AHHH!”

Those who were hit by the Buddhist Glory Illuminates the Universe fell down, splashing blood.

“Ackk! My arm!”

“Kuak....”

Those covered in the sharp plum blossom sword qi also let out desperate screams. The sword qi, fluttering lightly like petals, was too sharp for its appearance.

Even a simple brush from them would split the flesh, and a direct hit would break bones.

In such a space where so many people were crowded together, the vicious flower petals fully demonstrated their power.

No one else was watching.

“Protect the Namgung!”

At Baek Sang’s shout, the Mount Hua swordsmen kicked the ground with all their might. They rushed in like lightning, stabbing the retreating pirates who were terrified.

“Eukkk!”

Paaat!

Baek Sang’s sword swung out, digging into the center of a pirate’s chest. He incapacitated his opponent in one breath, immediately pulling out his sword and running towards the next pirate.

The pirate, spewing blood and falling, stabbed at him with a harpoon in a last desperate effort, but Baek Sang didn’t care.

Kakang!

Kwak Ho, following behind, knocked away the harpoon flying at Baek Sang.

He just had to do what he had to do. All that was left was to leave it to those who followed him. His sajaes and sajils who followed him were no less capable than him.

There wasn’t even a speck of reason not to trust them!

“You have to keep your head cool.”

At that moment, Baek Sang’s body trembled at the voice that came to his ear.

“Yes, sasuk!”

Swish!

Un Geom, who had easily dealt with one of the pirates targeting Baek Sang, looked around with cold eyes.

The disciples of Mount Hua, who received his gaze, fixed their swords and gradually calmed their boiling blood. Calmness returned to their eyes.

Tap.

At that moment, the five swords that had risen up came down as if guarding the four sides of the Namgung family.

“D-damn it! Why are you scared of their little numbers! We can trample them right now... Kuak!”

The raging water pirate couldn’t even finish his words before falling backward.

A deep blue dagger was stabbed into his forehead.

Of course, that wasn’t the end.

“Eukkkkk!”

“Ack! My face! My faceeee!”

Another barrage of spears and daggers caused part of the pirate circle to crumble. They were so dense that their shoulders were touching, leaving no way to escape the assassin’s weapons.

The pride of the pirates and their numbers had turned into a hindrance. To put it another way, from the Tang family’s perspective, there couldn’t have been an easier fight.

The pirates rushing ahead were all cut down by Mount Hua. They just needed to unleash their assassin’s weapon whenever they wanted.

In a battlefield where defense was not needed, the Tang family was at its most efficient.

“Hmm.”

Tang Gunak, who glared at the pirates with cold eyes like when he first met Chung Myung in the past, spat out.

“Show them what true suffering is like.”

“Yes!”

As soon as the Lord of the family gave the order, the Tang family’s warriors swung their hands even faster. Every time their hands emerged from their sleeves, desperate screams poured out from the pirates’ mouths.

The Namgung family’s swordsmen, who couldn’t believe the situation and couldn’t let their guard down, stared blankly at Mount Hua and the Tang family before finally collapsing.

“... they... are here....”

“Really... we had help coming...”

They couldn’t bring themselves to say, ‘We survived’ because they knew deep down that occupying this island was not the end.

They had to get off this island to truly survive.

But that was just what they were telling themselves in their heads; their hearts were saying something entirely different.

They turned their gaze towards Namgung Myung. No, more precisely, towards the person who had lost consciousness in Namgung Myung’s arms.

“....”

Looking at Namgung Do-Wei and remembering those who had fallen, no matter how much they tried to hold back the tears, their vision turned blurry.

“Young lord...”

So many sacrifices were made.

It was a small thing that he worked so hard his body turned into a rag. Namgung Do-Wei sacrificed even Namgung Hwang, the Lord of the family and his father, to save them all.

Thinking about the kind of thought he had in mind when he came running all the way there, he couldn’t dare to look him in the face.

And the people that Namgung Do-Wei had brought were now guarding them.

“Push!”

Mount Hua and the Tang family.

Who would have imagined that they would come running for the Namgung family? Even the Nine Great Sects had abandoned them. The Five Great Families, whom they had believed to be their blood allies, didn’t even show their faces on the island.

But Mount Hua, who had nothing to do with them, and the Tang family, who had broken away from the Five Great Families and turned to their enemies, entered Plum Blossom Island for them without any condition.

Even though they knew how dangerous the place was, they risked everything to save the Namgung family.

How on earth should they express this feeling?

“Stand up and hold your swords!”

At that moment, Namgung Myung grabbed Namgung Do-Wei and shouted.

“If you still have the strength to hold the sword, get up even if it means using your life! You are the warriors of the Namgung family. Are you going to sit back and wait because you received help? If sacrifices are needed, we should make the sacrifices, and if bleeding is needed, we should bleed! Don’t take their spirit for granted!”

Everyone came to their senses as if they had been struck by lightning at those words. The venom began to fill back into their eyes.

The Namgung family’s swordsmen, who had somehow managed to get up while trembling, moved ahead while staggering to join Mount Hua’s swordsmen surrounding them.

Chung Myung snickered at the sight.

‘Morons till the end.’

They were exhausted to death, and yet they didn’t sit down and hope for the others to help them out.

‘Well, this is Namgung.’

Since a long time back, the Namgung family has been the best in the world in terms of pride.

Chung Myung’s gaze coldly observed the battlefield. The heart of the battlefield was devouring people. Both the dominant and the inferior sides eventually got swept up in such a flow.

However, Chung Myung’s eyes were the only ones capable of capturing the entire battlefield with unparalleled sharpness.

This war was already over.

No, it had been over from the start.

Those who had been complacent, thinking there would be no salvation for Plum Blossom Island, could never defeat Mount Hua and the Tang family, who came rushing in.

Had they confronted each other in a place where they could exert their full strength, the pirates wouldn’t have even been a match for the Namgung family.

The pirates managed to win because they pushed the Namgung family to a place advantageous to them. And now, Plum Blossom Island was a battlefield favorable to Mount Hua and the Tang family.

All that was left was to just get swept away.

‘If not....’

Kwaaaak!

At that moment, an eerie cracking sound, like a giant rock rolling down a mountain, rang out.

Baek Cheon’s head turned violently to the side.

‘What is that?’

But all he could see were the thugs retreating in fear.

Yet Baek Cheon’s instincts were faster than his mind.

Thud!

The moment he suddenly kicked the ground and jumped up, the bodies of the pirates in front of him split apart and bounced off while the rough blade qi fiercely flew toward Mount Hua’s swordsmen.

“AHHHHHH!”

As if anticipating it, Baek Cheon blocked the path of the blade qi with his sword.

Kwaaaaaang!

Baek Cheon’s body, which had twisted and lifted the blade qi, flew back like a shot. Mount Hua’s disciples screamed as they saw him bounce into the air after hitting the ground once.

“Sasukkkk!”

“Sahyung! Damn it!”

Baek Cheon flipped in midair and landed on the ground.

Thud!

Baek Cheon landed on the floor as if sinking into it, stabbing his sword into the ground for support.

Red blood trickled down from his lips.

“This...”

Baek Cheon’s eyes were bloodshot. His physical condition was fine, but he was on the verge of losing his mind from anger.

“This fucking son...”

How... how could someone unleash a blade qi at the backs of his own subordinates?

“This...!”

Baek Cheon’s eyes clearly saw it.

The bodies of the pirates who had died in the attack a moment ago and the blood flowing from them formed a bright, bloody path.

At the end of the path between the pirates.

“... little bastards.”

The Black Dragon King, standing at the end and looking enraged, was approaching with a stride that seemed to crush the ground.

Chapter 973: Know That This Is Glory, Kid. (3)

No strategist could turn back the tide of a battlefield that had already collapsed.

If that were possible, why would strategists from ancient times study ways to escape with minimal casualties? It was common sense in strategy.

However, this was Kangho. It was a place where different elements of the general military strategy were at work.

Kuung!

The Black Dragon King took a step.

It was just a single step, but to those watching, it felt as if the Black Dragon King was crushing the land.

Overwhelming spirit and intimidation.

It was a battle that proved why such a person was called an absolute warrior.

The battlefield that had been swirling like a stormy sea suddenly stopped moving.

The pirates, the Tang family, and even Mount Hua.

They could only stare at the Black Dragon King walking on the blood-soaked ground as if they were possessed.

His appearance reminded them of a divine general who had descended from the heavens. But the voice that came out of his mouth was surely not divine.

“... fucking morons.”

The moment his seething voice flowed out of his mouth, the pirates all shrank their necks.

The absolute warrior can change the flow of the battlefield just by existing.

Without the Black Dragon King, the pirates were nothing more than a group of plunderers. However, the moment the Black Dragon King was added to them, they became part of the Shenzhou Five Sects and the Yangtze River’s 18 river families, one of the four in the Four Precepts Alliance.

“Huh!”

The Black Dragon King, who had been looking at his subordinates as if they were trash, fixed his gaze on Mount Hua.

“These little bastards dare to...”

Thud!

His feet trampled the ground as if he wanted to break it.

“You dare to act so fearlessly in front of me?”

His tone was no different from that of a ruffian.

However, the Black Dragon King demonstrated with his very presence that it wasn’t the tone or volume of his voice that commanded authority but his essence.

The voice, filled with incredible internal qi, rang out as if it would burst their eardrums.

The moment his crescent blade shook menacingly, Mount Hua’s swordsmen flinched and gripped their swords tightly.

They couldn’t even hear what he was saying.

What they felt was the terrifying aura emanating from the Black Dragon King.

‘Black Dragon King.’

Yoon Jong gritted his teeth.

He had already seen the Black Dragon up close in the past. But at that time, he had been too focused on Jang Ilso and the Ten Thousand People Clan to truly feel the presence of the Black Dragon King.

What could he say about the Black Dragon King standing right in front of him?

‘Rough.’

He felt an indescribable sense of discomfort.

That was the basic perception of those who wielded a sword.

Respect for the strong.

Those who honed themselves day by day and climbed the steep cliff of murim naturally came to respect those stronger than them.

This was because they could guess the effort and sweat that must have gone into achieving such skills. That was why they secretly hoped for it.

Absolute warrior.

Those who dared to add the word ‘absolute’ before their calling were said to possess the minimum level of dignity for it.

But...

‘No.’

The Black Dragon King was different.

This man did not even possess the minimum level of dignity that a warrior with the title ‘absolute’ should have. He was coercive, akin to a gangster who believed he could accomplish anything with his power.

But... Yoon Jong realized this anew.

How fearsome a person who ignored morality and law was.

He didn’t hesitate to show off his strength and didn’t find it difficult to coerce others. His extreme brutality even brought about nausea.

At the same time, he couldn’t help but realize how the man could perfectly control his large number of pirates just by his presence.

“You have done a great job.”

The mocking Black Dragon King shouted as if spitting fire.

“I will make you regret coming to this island on your own two feet in hell! I will tear you all to pieces and throw you as fish food!”

Clench.

At that moment, Baek Cheon raised his body while supporting the sword he had stuck in the ground.

“Spit!”

He spat out the blood that had filled his mouth and glared at the Black Dragon King with killing intent.

“At least if you are the lord of a sect.”

A voice flowed out as sharp as the blade.

“You should know how to protect those who will follow you, you bastard!”

Namgung Do-Wei, despite still being a young lord, risked everything he had to save his family. He didn’t even hesitate to throw his life into the river.

But the lord of the pirates threw his blade through the backs of his subordinates?

This was something Baek Cheon could never forgive. His anger surged.

He stood up to the Evil Sects and helped Namgung because he was in danger. To be blunt, his resentment was directed at Jang Ilso, not the Black Dragon King.

However, after witnessing such an unjust sight, Baek Cheon could never forgive the Black Dragon King.

“Huhahahahaha!”

But the Black Dragon King laughed loudly, looking at Baek Cheon.

“Right. There were quite a few. People like you who talk nonsense in front of me.”

Although he spoke slowly, his strong voice and force made it feel even more intimidating.

“What do you think happened to them?”

“This...”

Baek Cheon bit his lips tightly as if he would never be pushed back by the Black Dragon King’s force.

“They were all thrown into that blue river. That moron Emperor’s Sword or something.”

When Namgung Hwang’s name was mentioned to the survivors of the Namgung family, their eyes turned red. The sound of teeth grinding could be heard all around. It seemed like they would rush at the Black Dragon King at any moment.

But even with that great anger, they couldn’t move. They knew that rushing at the Black Dragon King in a fit of anger would be nothing more than a dog’s death. They understood that such a death would be an insult to the lord, who tried to protect them.

“Dogs who don’t know how scary the world is will bark as they want. Come here, you damned Taoist. I will tear that mouth of yours open!”

Tang Gunak furrowed his brows.

‘This isn’t good.’

To put it bluntly, their current situation wasn’t good. They had come to the island on their own feet and found themselves surrounded.

If they had kept their momentum till the end, they might have done things differently, but since the Black Dragon King’s appearance had stopped them, there was nothing left but a fierce fight.

There was just one way to regain their momentum.

‘Should I step forward?’

The burden on Mount Hua is heavy. Mount Hua had indeed become surprisingly strong, but facing the Black Dragon King alone would be another matter.

Especially on a battlefield like this where there could be no defeat.

What were the odds of winning?

No idea.

The results of those who have reached a certain level could only be known after competing. The only thing one could gain from it was glory or death.

Tang Gunak made up his mind. His martial arts weren’t complete yet. Given the Tang family’s weakness in close combat, fighting someone as strong as the Black Dragon King would surely be a burden on him.

But now was the time for him to step ahead. The lord of the Sichuan Tang family couldn’t just stay in this situation.

It was the moment he was about to step ahead.

“Ah, but these days, kids are....”

A voice suddenly came from somewhere, and Tang Gunak’s gaze turned.

Chung Myung slowly stepped forward, his sword glowing with blue qi, resting on his shoulder.

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“I’m saying this because I don’t understand.”

He walked with an indifferent expression and stood half a step in front of Baek Cheon.

“Why are you all shaking your heads like this? To someone who’s nothing?”

“C-Chung Myung.”

“....”

Baek Cheon looked at Chung Myung’s back, speechless.

Who in the world would dare say something like that in front of the Black Dragon King? This was something that neither the Shaolin abbot nor Jang Ilso would say.

‘But this kid is doing it.’

Because he was Chung Myung.

Right, he was Chung Myung.

And it seemed that even the Black Dragon King had heard of this guy’s infamy.

The Black Dragon King stared at Chung Myung, who had stepped forward and then spoke.

“You are a face I remember. Right, you were definitely that Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.”

“Ah, I know you too. You are that Black Dragon King.”

“....”

The Black Dragon King closed his mouth as if speechless for a moment, then burst into laughter.

“Kuahahahahaha!”

After laughing for a while, he shook his head and then suddenly turned serious.

“Kid, hiding your fear by using bragging isn’t a bad idea, but that doesn’t work on me. But don’t worry, if you get your head cut off and thrown into the river, you’ll be able to hide those pee-soaked pants.”

The Black Dragon King growled. Then Chung Myung snickered.

“The world has really turned into a better place.”

“... what?”

“This Evil Sect brat is even opening his mouth in front of me.”

The Black Dragon King’s eyes were bewildered at that.

After becoming a leader in the Shenzhou five sects and earning the title of Black Dragon King... no, even before that.

Has he ever heard someone say something like that?

“This...”

The Black Dragon King’s eyes began to shine with ferocious killing intent.

“This... a guy who doesn’t even know his place and thinks he is great just because he clashed with that guy, Jang Ilso, on the Yangtze River.”

“You shouldn’t talk about someone else’s past embarrassments. When I think about that time, I still kick the blanket off my bed, you bastard.”

“... what?”

Clench. Clench.

Chung Myung twisted his neck from side to side. He seemed upset, recalling bad memories.

“The more I think about it, the more I try to endure it.”

He snickered as if he found it funny.

“Even those insignificant guys talking nonsense now.”

“....”

“And I have to watch it.”

Chung Myung bared his teeth.

Today, Mount Hua proved how strong they had become on the Yangtze River. What they left behind on the Yangtze River would be passed down to the world through everyone’s mouths.

So now it was Chung Myung’s turn to show it.

What he had accomplished in the past three years.

“That is the end of the joke.”

The Black Dragon King took heavy steps toward Chung Myung, exuding a killing intent. Blade qi flowed like ink from the blade.

“You can all attack me at once. I will make sure you know why I am called the Black Dragon King.”

Soon, an incredible amount of power exploded from his body.

Everyone’s face turned pale at his power. The pirates retreated without even raising their heads properly due to the oppressive feeling that seemed to strangle their necks.

Regardless of the dignity, the Black Dragon King’s force as a warrior was clear. It was as if he had embodied the path of blade arts in his body.

Everyone couldn’t help but feel tired.

Except for one person.

“Well, are the Evil Sect bastards stupid? They just never seem to understand what I am saying.”

Only Chung Myung calmly flicked the sword on his shoulder opposite the Black Dragon King.

“It might be too much for an Evil Sect bastard, but I will teach you something special now.”

His lips twisted, revealing sharp fangs like those of a wolf.

“Know that it is glory, kid.”

Chung Myung slowly lowered his sword from his shoulder and aimed it at the Black Dragon King, who was acting wildly.

Chapter 974: Know That This Is Glory, Kid. (4)

Namgung Myung’s face, as he watched the situation, was filled with confusion.

‘What now?’

Who was fighting whom?

His eyes trembled as he stared at the back of the man standing in front of everyone.

Of course, this man was different from the typical skilled warriors of sects he was familiar with. He, too, acknowledged that.

Not just Namgung Myung, but in the current murim world, there would be no one who considered Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, a common skilled warrior. If there was still someone like that in this murim world, they would either be an idiot who doesn’t understand how the world works or a fool envious of someone’s shining reputation.

Even Namgung Do-Wei, considered one of the top skilled warriors of sects and objectively possessing the potential to be one of the best lords of the Namgung family, would appear shabby compared to Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.

Mount Hua’s Sword Saint was on a different level.

But...

Namgung Myung’s gaze moved past him and landed on the Black Dragon King.

‘That depends on the opponent too, right?’

Everything in the world was relative. Just as Namgung Do-Wei’s fame and skills paled in front of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, even more so did Mount Hua’s Sword Saint’s fame seemed diminished before the Black Dragon King’s name.

It was only natural.

The title of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint simply embodied respect for the sword technique he had demonstrated and the expectations for where his sword would reach in the future. If he had to add one more thing, it would be praise for the path Chung Myung took.

But not the Black Dragon King.

He was not respected by the world but was someone who had built a reputation that everyone in the world recognized just by how many he had killed.

Namgung Myung glared at the Black Dragon King with red eyes.

Even the lord of the Namgung family, Emperor’s Sword Namgung Hwang, was unable to gain the upper hand in the fierce battle with the Black Dragon King. No, didn’t he end up surrendering to the clutches of the Black Dragon King?

Although Namgung Myung hated the Black Dragon King more than anyone else, there was no denying that the Black Dragon King was one of the strongest men in the world.

But...

Was Mount Hua’s Sword Saint really going to face the Black Dragon King?

‘This is beyond insane.’

No matter how many times he thought about it, the only image he could conjure was Mount Hua’s Sword Saint being torn to shreds by the blade of the Black Dragon King.

‘But why isn’t anyone stopping him! Just why!’

Even though Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, who was only one of the Third Class disciples, went out to face the Black Dragon King alone, none of Mount Hua’s disciples tried to stop him.

They just lowered their swords and silently stood behind him.

“Ugh....”

Namgung Myung made a judgment based on common sense. This was the Mount Hua sect, incredibly strong and full of determination, but they still lacked the experience to understand the power of the Black Dragon King.

An absolute warrior was by no means common. It would not be strange if Mount Hua, who had lost the precious glory that should have existed, did not comprehend the power of the Black Dragon King.

But if that were the case...

Namgung Myung looked at Tang Gunak.

‘Why is the lord silent?’

If there was anyone who should stand up against the Black Dragon King here, it would naturally be Tang Gunak. Of course, the prestige of the Poison King was slightly inferior to the great name of the Black Dragon King, but even so, wasn’t he the head of the Tang family? At least, he had the same ‘level’ as the Black Dragon King.

However, even though he must have clearly felt Namgung Myung’s gaze, Tang Gunak just watched the confrontation between Chung Myung and the Black Dragon King with a blank expression.

Namgung Myung grew more and more confused.

They really intended to use Mount Hua’s Sword Saint as the opponent to the Black Dragon King.

‘No.’

That could not happen.

That man would definitely become a symbol to the murim world in the future. They couldn’t just let someone like that die here at the behest of such a ruthless person.

At least Namgung Myung was not shameless enough to watch the death of someone who risked his life for them.

“Someone....”

Namgung Myung gritted his teeth and tried to step forward. There was no way he could be a match for the Black Dragon King, but if he fought without backing down, he might be able to turn the tide of the battlefield a little.

But it was right then.

Tak.

Someone grabbed him by the shoulder.

“...y-young lord?”

Namgung Myung turned around in surprise and saw Namgung Do-Wei, who had come to his senses, grabbing his shoulder. Namgung Do-Wei opened his pale lips.

“No.”

“... What?”

“Just watch.”

He covered his mouth and coughed, wiping the blood from his mouth with his sleeve. His sharp eyes focused on Chung Myung’s back.

Namgung Myung looked at him with a blank expression.

He could see the firm trust in his eyes. He blankly looked around, and only then did he see everyone’s eyes.

‘They are not just watching....’

They were all like Namgung Do-Wei. Their eyes were free of any doubt. Everyone was looking at him with eyes of trust.

Not just the disciples of Mount Hua but also the Tang family and even Tang Gunak.

“Just how...”

What did they even see in Mount Hua’s Sword Saint?

What did they see that made them trust him without even a shred of doubt in this situation? And against that Black Dragon King.

“... are you saying that he will defeat the Black Dragon King?”

“Yes.”

Namgung Myung’s lips trembled at the unbelievable answer.

“Have you seen the man’s skills?”

“... I don’t know precisely.”

“Then how...?”

Namgung Do-Wei smiled faintly.

“Because they are watching.”

He was referring to the Five Swords and the Mount Hua disciples.

They had only been together on a short journey, but he could feel how much they cared for one another. They wouldn’t hesitate to give up their lives for each other.

And yet, these people were looking at Chung Myung without a waver in their eyes.

Then what could he doubt Chung Myung for?

‘Can I be like that too?’

Namgung Do-Wei looked at the back of Chung Myung, standing firmly in front of everyone.

Would he ever be able to lead people with just his presence and not a sword, like Chung Myung?

He clenched his fists slowly, then stared at Chung Myung’s back as if engraving it into his memory.

The wind of the Yangtze River blew between Chung Myung and the Black Dragon King.

The scene surrounding them had changed quite strangely.

Those standing behind the Black Dragon King were all keeping their distance from him. Even though no one had told them to, they had been slowly moving back on their own and naturally went far.

But the place where Chung Myung stood was different.

Those standing behind Chung Myung did not retreat even a single step from where they had first been. They were all glaring at the Black Dragon King from behind as if they were going to fight together.

The Black Dragon King held his sword tightly.

‘I don’t like this.’

The sight of the kid stepping ahead to fight was scratching his pride.

In addition, the eyes of the people behind him were also unpleasant.

Did they really think the young brat could be an opponent for him?

‘Well, this is good.’

The feeling was secondary.

The Black Dragon King knew how important the flow of the battlefield was. Those young swordsmen of Mount Hua were stronger than he thought, and the Tang family was always a difficult opponent.

If they gained momentum, even if they were to annihilate them, the damage to the pirates would increase exponentially. In order to minimize the damage, he had to completely break down their morale.

They had to show off how strong he was in front of them and break off their will to fight back. And in order to do that...

‘Just killing him won’t do.’

The Black Dragon King’s eyes, filled with a vicious aura, looked at him.

He would tear that bastard who dared to come forward to pieces and show everyone who he was. Not just them, but also Jang Ilso, who was leisurely watching from the distant ship!

“You will regret in hell for daring to stand before me...”

That was the moment.

Chung Myung’s form, who had been glaring at him with the sword pointed, disappeared from sight in an instant.

‘What?’

Reflexively drawing up the internal qi, the Black Dragon King drew the sword and covered his neck. It was in instinctive action created by countless battle experiences.

And that one move saved the Black Dragon King.

Kwaaang!

The Black Dragon King’s crescent blade barely blocked Chung Myung’s sword, which moved like a ghost.

Kakakg! Kakakagak! Kakakak!

A chilling sound echoed as the thin sword pushed against the incredibly thick blade.

“This...”

The eyes of the Black Dragon King fluttered.

If he had delayed his reaction by even a moment, that sword would have surely sliced his neck. But what was making the Black Dragon King’s entire body sweat wasn’t the sense of crisis that his entire life was in danger.

‘What is this guy?’

As he pushed ahead roughly as if he was going to cut off the Black Dragon King’s throat, Chung Myung’s expression was eerily indifferent.

Goosebumps ran down the Black Dragon King’s spine as he observed, feeling an inexplicable chill.

Kakakakak!

The sword, with tremendous force, pressed down on his blade. Startled by the pain in his twisted wrist, the Black Dragon King let out a beastly roar.

“Ahhhhhh!”

Reflexively, he drew up his qi, pushing the sword forward with all his might.

But at that moment, the Black Dragon King saw it.

Just before he pushed the sword away, Chung Myung’s sword moved aside precisely as if he anticipated the move.

As if time had slowed down, he saw everything clearly.

His sword pushed with all his might, was now moving in vain, striking only empty space where the sword no longer existed.

Chung Myung, having easily let his strength dissipate, closed in on the Black Dragon King’s side.

The moment he saw those eyes, dazzling because they held no emotion, the Black Dragon King stopped thinking and threw his body to the side with all his might.

Kwang!

It was a technique of incredible speed, unmatched by his large body.

In the blink of an eye, he moved more than ten steps away, thick beads of sweat running down his chin.

“...”

He raised one hand blankly, looking at his left hand, now covered in white sand.

While jumping, his hand had touched the ground, and the sweat on it had filled his palm with traces of humiliation, sticking the sand to it.

The Black Dragon King, staring at his hand as if possessed, then looked at his side. His robe, a symbol of his status, and the armor beneath it were neatly split open as if cut from the start.

And...

Drip.

The cut between them was wide enough to fit a finger.

The Black Dragon King raised his head and saw the back of the human who had lowered his sword.

Before he could fully register the discomfort creeping into his mind and the fear emanating from the human, Chung Myung slowly turned to face the Black Dragon King.

“You are unlucky.”

His cold voice pierced the silence. However, even colder than Chung Myung’s voice was his gaze as he stared at the Black Dragon King.

“You could have died without pain.”

A drop of sweat ran down the Black Dragon King’s chin and fell onto the blood pooled on the ground.

Tak.

That faint sound echoed across Plum Blossom Island, which was enveloped in a terrible silence.

Chapter 975: Know That This Is Glory, Kid. (5)

The side where the sword had cut him started to sting, and soon, a terrible pain surged through him.

The Black Dragon King was by no means a fool. His actions and harsh words were somewhat intentional. Contrary to his external appearance, his reasoning was mostly cold, and he faced reality.

But now, if it weren’t for the sharp pain in his side, he would have wondered if this situation was a dream.

‘I couldn’t see it.’

That bastard’s form, that sword.

‘Was I careless?’

That wasn’t even funny.

The Black Dragon King had decided to give it his all. In a situation where so many people were watching, he had to show an overwhelming presence and turn the flow of the battlefield toward the pirates. Careless behavior was out of the question in this task.

And yet, he couldn’t see it. Not a bit.

‘How though...’

Cold sweat ran down his back.

Then, that meant the kid’s speed really exceeded the range he could perceive, and he was just barely past his teens?

‘That makes no sense!’

That was impossible!

It was even harder to accept because the Black Dragon King wasn’t stupid.

Everything in the world has its limits.

A tiger that could tear apart a bear as big as a house and crush rocks could exist. Maybe once in a thousand years, once in ten thousand years, such a tiger would appear.

But wasn’t a tiger that flew already beyond the classification of being a ‘tiger’? People could imagine a tiger that jumps, but they could not imagine a tiger that flew in the air. That was ‘common sense’.

The idea that he could accept the skills of Chung Myung as they were now was no different from believing that there existed a tiger that could fly in the sky and also drop lightning.

Throb! Throb!

His wound and instincts were screaming at him to retreat from this opponent.

But his reason was screaming that he should never show weakness here.

In the fierce battle between instinct and reason, the Black Dragon King had no idea how to react.

Wheik!

Chung Myung, who had turned his sword and was holding it, walked towards the Black Dragon King.

Flinch.

The Black Dragon King desperately grabbed his feet, which were trying to move back without realizing it. The Black Dragon King of this world would retreat against such a brat? Even if he tore Chung Myung to pieces now, this would be a shame that would never leave him.

‘What are you thinking!’

He gritted his teeth.

What did it matter whether the opponent was strong or not?

He couldn’t retreat anyway. The moment he backed down, he would lose the right to be called the Black Dragon King. The pirates would be torn apart, and he would live his whole life being pointed at.

It was alright for the Evil Sects to be cutthroat, but he must never be a laughingstock.

Clench!

The Black Dragon King steeled himself and slammed the blade hard into the ground. Then, gritting his teeth, he held the sword with both hands and aimed at Chung Myung.

“Euk!”

Forget everything.

How strong he was in the past, how he became strong in just three years. That all doesn’t matter in this spar.

All he had to think about was one thing: how to kill him!

“BRATTTTT!”

When you didn’t know the strength of the opponent, the best you could do was defend. However, the Black Dragon King instead charged forward. He judged that he shouldn’t get caught up in the opponent’s flow.

The sight of him explosively charging ahead with the huge crescent blade was powerful enough to take the breath away from the opposite person. The ink-colored qi, which erupted from the tip of the blade, drew a line in the air, capturing everyone’s attention.

However, his momentum seemed to have no effect on Chung Myung at all. Even as he watched the Black Dragon King fiercely charging ahead, Chung Myung’s eyes were no different from the start of the match.

Even the way he walked toward the Black Dragon King showed no change. It was as if his eyes couldn’t see the Black Dragon King.

Kwaang!

The extended foot rushing forward shook the entire island. With such a powerful step on the ground, blood gushed from his waist. The Black Dragon King pulled out all of his qi without waste and brought down the blade he had twisted and raised with all his might.

Paaaat!

At that sound, goosebumps ran down everyone’s bodies.

It wasn’t the force of the internal qi. His internal qi was not being released outside at all but was contained entirely in the blade. So, the only thing creating that hollow, low sound was the body of the Black Dragon King.

His body, trained countless times, spun around fiercely with elasticity like rubber and completely contained its qi in the blade. Just through that process, his body was creating a storm.

And the blade, which held all the strength of the body and the internal qi, fell as if it would split heaven and earth in two.

Even Baek Cheon, who was watching, had to clench his fist and tighten his entire body’s muscles as the incredible blade rushed toward Chung Myung’s head. It was an incredible speed.

‘Bastard!’

But at that moment, a sense of doubt filled the Black Dragon King’s eyes.

The blade was falling as if it would shatter Chung Myung’s head, but he didn’t show any reaction. He didn’t make any moves to dodge or block the incoming attack.

He just stared blankly at the Black Dragon King and the falling blade with eyes that seemed to be observing.

And that was the moment.

The sword that had been hanging down suddenly shot up like lightning, all toward the blade falling toward Chung Myung’s head.

The Black Dragon King reflexively amplified the amount of internal qi that was being pushed back in.

Kuuung!

That was the sound that resonated not from the outside but from within. The moment the blade and the sword clashed, the shock, which could be described as dull, dug into his insides.

But that was it. The power in that sword was too weak to block his blade.

‘Just that!’

He put strength into the blade he was holding as if he was going to split Chung Myung from head to toe in an instant.

As expected, Chung Myung’s sword, which was clinging to the blade, bounced back helplessly like a leaf caught in a storm.

But at that very moment.

Paaat!

The sword that had bounced away flew back in like a ray of light and collided with the Black Dragon King’s sword.

Kuung! Kuung! Kuung!

A dull and loud thud erupted from inside the Black Dragon King’s ears, not outside but inside.

The source of that thud was none other than the blade he was holding. The blade was vibrating at an incredible speed, making his entire body tremble like a large drum.

‘T-this is...!’

The Black Dragon King clearly saw it.

The sight of the sword, which was nothing more than a sword but could never be considered a mere blade, drawing numerous trajectories in the short distance between the blade and Chung Myung.

It was like a meteor shower. A meteor shower that rose from the ground to the sky.

Like the flickering of stars in the night sky, the sword qi, which drew fantastic trajectories, was literally striking the blade of the Black Dragon King.

The qi contained in each sword was incomparable to that of the Black Dragon King. However, before the qi from the first sword strike had even dissipated, the second sword strike struck the blade, and before the qi could even be transmitted into the body, the third one came rushing in.

Dozens of consecutive strikes followed like that.

The moment when the qi of the consecutive strikes, which seemed to split into dozens, gathered at the center of the sword, everyone’s mouth dropped open.

Kwaaang!

The Black Dragon King’s blade bounced back.

The nine-foot-long crescent blade—it was the Black Dragon King himself, not someone else, who swung it with all his might. Yet, it bounced off a thin sword, and it felt unrealistic to him.

However, the one who felt the shock the most was none other than the Black Dragon King. His eyes widened as if they would tear.

Kwaaang!

Before the shock even reached his mind, the Black Dragon King kicked the ground. Since the blade had lost control, his instincts knew it was dangerous to remain at this distance.

But his opponent was Chung Myung.

Almost simultaneously with his retreat, Chung Myung followed him like a ghost.

Slash!

Soon, Chung Myung’s sword cut open the Black Dragon King’s thick pectoralis major muscle with almost absurd ease. He could clearly feel his chest being torn apart.

But there was something more horrifying than that.

The moment he saw the distorted eyes and the corners of his lips curling, the newly revealed white qi made the Black Dragon King feel as if his blood had gone cold.

Unable to hold back, the Black Dragon King let out a scream as if he were losing his mind.

“EUHHHHHH!”

The blade, which had bounced back, rushed towards Chung Myung’s head.

But at that moment, Chung Myung’s sword pierced the center of the Black Dragon King’s wrist, which was holding the blade.

Clench!

The steel-like muscles were torn, and the sharp metal dug into the bones. But more than the cold sensation, this bitter coldness pierced the Black Dragon King’s chest.

But.

“OHHHHH!”

The Black Dragon King was the Black Dragon King. He swung the crescent blade without paying attention to the sword embedded in his wrist as if he would split Chung Myung, even if it meant cutting off his wrist.

But at that moment, the Black Dragon King had to know.

There must be someone more insane than him in the world.

Clench!

Something blocked the crescent blade’s path, which hadn’t even swung halfway. The Black Dragon King opened his eyes wide after confirming the identity of the object that blocked the crescent blade.

‘Hand?’

Chung Myung, who had swung the sword with great skill and stabbed the Black Dragon King’s wrist, grabbed the Black Dragon King’s crescent blade with his left hand.

Naturally, his hand split open, and blood flowed down the cut part. However, Chung Myung’s eyes, visible through the blood, remained cold and sunken.

Clench!

Without any hesitation, the sword slipped out of the Black Dragon King’s wrist and immediately unleashed dozens of sword forms.

The Black Dragon King instinctively tried to pull the long blade. However, the hand that was stuck to the blade did not let go easily, as if it had been glued.

‘This fucking dog!’

The Black Dragon King drew out his internal qi and unfolded the blade. The hand holding the blade was swept away by the blade qi and began to tear apart.

But the Black Dragon King also had to pay a price for it.

Slash!

A vivid line was engraved on his upper arm.

Slash!

The sword form that dug into his thigh cut off all his muscles there.

Puaak!

The sword that dug into his side made a black hole the size of a child’s fist in his steel-like abs.

Intense pain began to spread throughout his body.

“U-ughhhhh!”

He could clearly see the sword digging into his stomach. The Black Dragon King’s eyes were bloodshot. Gritting his teeth, he concentrated his internal qi into his stomach. Then, he thrust the blade towards Chung Myung.

‘If only there was no sword!’

Looking at the blade flying towards his face, Chung Myung smiled bitterly.

A moment later, something the Black Dragon King had never imagined happened.

Chung Myung let go of his sword, which was stuck in the Black Dragon King’s stomach.

‘What?’

The Black Dragon King was shocked, his heart nearly leaping into his throat.

Chung Myung extended his battered hand and seized the blade the Black Dragon King wielded. He could see the flesh on his hand tearing and scattering as it struggled to withstand the attack.

But in the next moment, the Black Dragon King witnessed Chung Myung’s knee flying toward his face.

Kwaaang!

The Black Dragon King fell, blood streaming from him.

As he flew like a broken kite and crashed into the sand mound, a dead silence enveloped the entire island.

“Cough!”

The Black Dragon King convulsed violently and spat out blood.

His upper body shivering, he saw Chung Myung’s sword embedded in his stomach.

“... Insane... utterly insane...”

Where in the world would you find such a person?

Even the Evil Sect didn’t fight like that. No matter how vicious someone was, they wouldn’t fight by sacrificing their own body in such a manner.

This kid was literally insane.

Through his blurred vision from the shock, he saw Chung Myung looking down at his ravaged left hand. He gazed at his hand indifferently without uttering a word, then wiped the blood flowing down as if it were merely an annoyance.

“Sword.”

As soon as Chung Myung spoke, a white sword flew to him. He reached out and caught the flying sword, grinning.

“Nice.”

It was the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword, which had been initially thrown onto the island. Jo Gul had been quick to retrieve it.

Chung Myung nodded, satisfied with the feel of the sword in his hand.

After retrieving the sword, he turned to the Black Dragon King and moved.

Tap. Tap.

With each step, blood dripped from his wrist onto the white sand. It was a horrifying sight in its own right.

“Get up.”

A voice colder than the northern wind flowed from Chung Myung’s mouth.

“If not, don’t speak nonsense with a hole in your body.”

‘Do you know about war? You?’

A twisted smile formed on his lips.

“I will tell you. Not about the war you play around with, but what real war actually is.”

Those who saw the madness in Chung Myung’s eyes at that moment lowered their gazes without realizing it.

Chapter 976: There Is No Way I Wouldn't Believe. (1)

The phrase ‘the sound of a flowing river’ was actually a bit awkward.

A stream flowed like a sword, but few could hear the sound of a deer river flowing. The river simply flowed powerfully.

However, those standing on Plum Blossom Island clearly heard the sound of the river flowing for a moment. It was a sound like a steady rhythm created by the waves meeting one another.

Such terrible silence had descended on the island.

One couldn’t believe it even if they saw it with their own eyes.

A third-class disciple of Mount Hua, a young swordsman who had barely passed his prime, was now cornering the Black Dragon King, who had established himself as an absolute warrior in Kangho, a place where blades formed mountains and the swords sprouted like forests.

When viewed objectively, the extent of the injuries the two people sustained wasn’t much different.

Of course, the Black Dragon King’s body had cuts and puncture wounds. But considering his large body and strength, such injuries wouldn’t be called fatal.

On the other hand, Chung Myung’s injury was limited to one hand, but that hand now looked damaged to the point where it was almost impossible to move.

The flesh on the back of his hand was almost torn off, revealing his bones, and the palm was a mess as if it had been shredded.

If someone were to arrive at the scene and examine the two, they would have a hard time determining who had the upper hand.

But those who were there, those who had watched the spar from the beginning to the present with their own eyes, would dare not call this match a ‘draw’.

A terrible sense of discomfort had gripped everyone. In front of such a scene, where the common sense that had been taken for granted was collapsing, even if they wanted to speak, they could only stay silent.

“....”

Namgung Myung, who was about to speak, closed his mouth, unable to find the words. It seemed impossible to express his current feelings in any language.

He glanced down at his hands, noticing the cold sweat trickling down his palms.

‘Just who is that person...’

He couldn’t comprehend it. No, it was all terrifying.

Chung Myung’s swordplay was meticulously calculated. Namgung Myung, as a member of the Namgung family—a lineage renowned for never losing the title of the best swordsman in the world—could recognize it instantly.

How much effort and determination were poured into that single swing that seemed so effortless?

Even if one didn’t bear the Namgung name, anyone skilled with a sword would understand.

But could mere effort explain such a spectacle? No matter how much they pushed themselves, no matter how finely they honed their blade, could they fight like this?

‘Impossible.’

That wasn’t something anyone could learn. It wasn’t achievable through mere intention.

Chung Myung was fundamentally different.

Namgung Myung didn’t know what made Chung Myung that way, but witnessing him wield the sword sent shivers down his spine. His jaw trembled, and his blood felt cold.

That was when it happened.

“That damned human...”

“Tsk.”

An irritated voice emerged from the Five Swords standing behind him. No, rather than irritation, it was more accurate to say they were angry.

Their gaze was fixed on Chung Myung.

But then, a peculiar sight unfolded. Chung Myung, who had captivated Namgung Myung to the point of breathlessness, was tearing off his clothes.

Tsk!

Chung Myung wrapped his injured hand with the cloth he had torn.

“Tsk.”

It was impossible to take care of one’s own body while chasing the enemy. Even though he had been told repeatedly that it was more important not to get hurt than to kill the enemy, he had never once tended to his own injuries while fighting.

But now, he was wrapping his hand with his own clothes like a bandage, even if it was an awkward wrap. That was because he could see the eyes glaring at him as if they wanted to kill him.

‘Damned bastards.’

Baek Cheon, Jo Gul, and Yoo Jong were glaring, and Yu Yiseol’s expression didn’t change, but she looked like she might rush in and strangle Chung Myung at any moment. In particular, the face of Tang Soso, who was radiating killing intent behind her, was too terrifying for any human to look at.

Chung Myung snickered as he tied up his injured hand.

If Tang Bo had seen this, he might have frowned and even passed out.

Clench!

Chung Myung, who had pulled the cloth off and wrapped his hand with it, spat out the sand that stuck to his mouth and fixed the sword in his hand. Then, thinking about it again, he smiled faintly as if he couldn’t believe it.

At that moment, the Black Dragon King raised his large body.

Blood was flowing down his lips, which were clenched tightly to hold back his anger. His crescent blade, which had hit the ground, trembled.

“This...”

At that moment, a frenzied qi radiated from the Black Dragon King’s entire body.

The force felt so overwhelming that some of the pirates even fainted and fell down. The pressure was so tremendous that even the disciples of Mount Hua flinched.

“You fucking rat!”

The eyes of the Black Dragon King turned red.

What did all this mean?

He would acknowledge the speed. But none of the injuries he sustained while fighting that damned bastard were due to his ‘skill’.

It was the result of his courage to push ahead without taking care of his own injuries, his ridiculous improvisation in the fights, and the madness of that insane guy.

He cursed outwardly, but his face was filled with caution.

‘That guy is extremely familiar with such fights.’

Actually, the word ‘extreme’ was too crude to describe what he had just experienced. Even he, who usually dismissed learning, felt bitter that he couldn’t find the words to express the insanity and venom of that moment.

A normal warrior waited for his opponent’s opening. A warrior at his peak creates an opening in his opponent’s defense with his own strength.

But this guy didn’t stop there.

He was forcibly twisting and creating gaps, inserting his claws and fangs into them, tearing open any wound he could.

Step. Step.

He stared at Chung Myung, who was approaching, pulling his clothes tight with his teeth.

Even the sound of his steps felt oppressive.

Clench.

The Black Dragon King gritted his teeth.

No rabbit approached a tiger first. At least, being able to approach first in a battle was clear evidence of having the upper hand.

The situation was one where the Black Dragon King’s high and proud pride would be torn apart, but instead of cursing and yelling loudly as usual, he slowly aimed the crescent blade ahead.

His actions were infinitely cautious.

It symbolized acknowledgment. This meant he accepted this young swordsman in front of him as a great opponent he had to fight with his life on the line.

No strong warrior was strong from the beginning. Didn’t he survive among those stronger than him and come this far?

Instead of sweeping around like a storm, a sharp, killing intent like a blade began to flow through him.

The corners of Chung Myung’s lips curled up.

“Now it is time to do it right.”

“Don’t be so cocky. Kid...”

The Black Dragon King couldn’t finish his words. It seemed as if someone had shut his mouth. He couldn’t bring himself to call that insane Taoist a kid anymore.

“Kuak.”

The Black Dragon King’s eyes darkened as he closed his mouth, crushing his lips together.

He knew.

The result justified everything. The stronger the man, the greater the praise he will receive when he finally wins.

And so...

The Black Dragon King, catching his breath, slowly pushed away the sand with his feet. The sword naturally rose and fell with his breathing.

His eyes, which had been wild like a boar struck by a preemptive blow, slowly changed to those of a warrior.

Swish!

As his feet kicked the ground, the black image of the Black Dragon King shot ahead like an illusion. At the same time as the sand that had been torn apart rose, the Black Dragon King’s body reached Chung Myung’s face.

Wheik!

Short and quick stabs followed.

This was not an attack led only by the blade. A long blade, longer than a spear, executed stabs that took full advantage of its crescent shape. Even before the first stab had reached its full extension, the afterimages of the blade split into attacks that swept over Chung Myung’s entire body without losing force.

Chung Myung quickly retreated. However, at that moment, the afterimages of the Black Dragon King’s sword extended even further, chasing after him.

“Hum!”

With a short hum, Chung Myung extended his sword like a poisonous snake flying through the air.

Kakang!

Chung Myung’s sword accurately dug through the gap between the blade forms, bouncing back and forth to push them away. It was the very moment when Chung Myung was about to swing his sword through the gap he had created by squeezing the space.

Wheeing!

With a whistling sound, the Black Dragon King’s blade surged through the space created by Chung Myung like a ray of light. Chung Myung instantly twisted his hand and raised his sword vertically.

Kaaang!

The blade and sword collided in the air, and Chung Myung’s body was thrown back.

At that moment, the Black Dragon King fixed his blade.

Kwaang!

As his feet struck the ground with such force it seemed they might crush it, the blade rose fiercely once more.

Kwaaaak!

From the tip of the blade, a cascade of ink-colored blade qi poured out. It was as if a giant brush had drawn a single stroke in the air!

Against the blade flying towards him as if it would shatter him, Chung Myung twisted his body in the air and pulled his sword back behind his back.

“Ahhh!”

The red crescent-shaped sword qi emitted from the tip of Chung Myung’s sword, which was swung like a whip. The sword qi, resembling the light of the setting sun, collided head-on with the flying black qi.

KWAAANNNGGGG!

The moment the two qis clashed, a huge explosion of qi occurred in the air with a tremendous roar, as if a cannon had been fired.

Thud!

At that moment, the black qi at the tip of the Black Dragon King’s blade swept out. In an instant, the black qi, which had grown to the size of a house, condensed and condensed again, forming a single line. Then, it wrapped around the sword and began to spin fiercely.

“HOOOOO!”

The Black Dragon King roared at the top of his lungs. Then the qi rose higher, and the crescent sword was completely engulfed in the black blade qi, which was spinning.

KWAAANG!

He swung the blade as if tearing apart the air. The enhanced blade qi extended from the blade, drawing out nine lines.

Nine Dragon’s March!

The Nine Dragon’s March seemed to be flying towards Chung Myung.

The first screams came from the mouths of the Mount Hua disciples who were watching that magnificent sight.

“Chung Myungggg!”

But at that moment, a red dot appeared in the air.

The blue sky and the clear blue river. In that world that was like a canvas, red flowers bloomed like clouds.

Explosive bloom.

Plum Blossoms bloomed in the tens, hundreds, and thousands, creating a huge wall in the air. The world turned red as if the sunset was spreading across the deep blue sky in an instant.

Soon, the Nine Dragons entangled themselves and collided head-on with the wall of red plum blossoms blocking them.

Kwaaang!

With a huge explosion, as if a mountain was collapsing, red and black qi exploded like firecrackers and flew away.

Chapter 977: There Is No Way I Wouldn't Believe. (2)

It was a truly magnificent sight.

The black and red shards of qi spread out in all directions like firecrackers as if giant red and black flowers were blooming in the air. The sight was not only magnificent but almost mesmerizing at the same time.

But it was also extremely dangerous.

“A-avoid it!”

“Ackkkk!”

The explosive qi covered Plum Blossom Island at an incredible speed and flew away. Each piece of the blade qi, as sharp as a sword, rained down like a storm.

The pirates were helplessly pierced by the sword qi.

Since each of them was not of a high level, there was no way for them to avoid the qi pouring down from above their heads.

“Ack!”

“My arm! My ARMMM!”

The pirates, pierced all around, were screaming in despair.

But the situation on the other side, for Mount Hua, was entirely different.

“Protect the Namgung!”

“Yes!”

As soon as Hyung Jong gave the order, the swordsmen of Mount Hua jumped into the air and repelled the flying sword qi. In the process, the plum blossoms that bloomed dyed one side of the island red.

“Hmm!”

Tang Gunak also swung his dagger and struck out the blade qi. Then he stared blankly at Mount Hua, who was blocking and protecting him.

The Tang family was a sect with clear strengths and weaknesses, as its nature suggested. It was better than any other sect in attacking the enemies but was extremely vulnerable to defense due to their sect’s feature of using assassination weapons, which were short.

Mount Hua already knew that and was protecting not just the Namgung family but also the Tang family.

Such an odd feeling.

Who would have thought the day would come when a sect with past glories would start protecting them?

Tang Gunak’s gaze went beyond the swordsmen of Mount Hua and went far toward the two people who were starting to clash again through their explosive qi.

He could clearly see the sword and the blade clashing.

Kaaang!

Every time they clashed and fell, there was a deafening noise. And then, as soon as they fell, they clashed fiercely again.

Kakakakak!

The sword and the blade, which had been pushing each other back strongly, collided in an instant.

“AHHH!”

The Black Dragon King’s crescent blade split the air. The black-colored blade sent a straight line of qi towards Chung Myung.

A single, honest blow with no deviation. But that was why it was fast and even more destructive.

Flutter!

The tip of Chung Myung’s sword began to tremble madly. The endless stream of plum blossoms pushed aside the flying dark blade qi.

KWAANGGGG!

The blade qi crashed into the ground, and sand shot up into the air like a fountain. If someone had seen the deep scar left on the ground later, they would never have believed it was a mark made by a blade.

“Kuak!”

However, there was no sign of victory on the face of the Black Dragon King, who had created this great mark.

“Woahh!”

The Black Dragon King swung his blade, his throat about to burst, one after another. The dozens of blade qi he launched flew toward Chung Myung, aiming to engulf him.

Each of them had enough power to crush a large number of pirate ships, yet the Black Dragon King unleashed them as if breathing.

He had truly become the leader of a great sect like the Yangtze River Pirates with just his strength. It was truly an amazing strength.

Like ink spreading on a canvas, the blade qi spread out clearly for Chung Myung.

Pat!

However, even after seeing that terrifying sight, Chung Myung didn’t step back but instead jumped towards the blade qi flying at him.

The moment the ink-colored blade qi was about to cover him, Chung Myung, who had been rushing towards them, disappeared from the spot as if extinguished.

No, he did not disappear.

Chung Myung bent down as much as he could, digging under the blade qi. He stabbed his sword into the ground and kicked his feet simultaneously, his body moving faster than before.

Swish!

The blade qi passed right over Chung Myung’s head. Severed hair scattered in all directions. If it had been even an inch off, his head would have been cut off. Yet, Chung Myung, rushing ahead, showed no hesitation.

A vivid killing intent flowed from his eyes as he flew like a swallow over water.

The Black Dragon King didn’t miss the chance and leaped forward in a breath. At the same time, his blade forcefully struck Chung Myung, who had lowered his body.

The incredible qi of that path seemed enough to turn everything in its way to dust.

But at that moment, Chung Myung’s eyes shone.

Psst!

He kicked the ground and soared towards the descending blade. Then, he placed one hand on the sword’s body and pushed it up with both hands to block the approaching blade with the sword’s flat side.

The Black Dragon King’s eyes shone with anger upon seeing that sight.

‘This fucking dog-like bastard!’

He squeezed out all his strength and pushed into the blade. He pressed down with all his might, determined to cut down Chung Myung right there.

Kwaaang!

The Black Dragon King saw it clearly. The sword blocking his blade was bent as if it would break at any moment. Blood splashed from Chung Myung’s palm, which pressed against the sword’s body, and his wrist was slowly twisting.

His back bent slightly backward as if it would break, and blood dripped from his mouth.

It all happened in a split second.

But his blade, not yet finished with its attack, kept going. Just by pressing down like this, he would finally kill the kid by splitting him in two!

‘Die!’

It was the very moment when the Black Dragon King was trying to punish Chung Myung’s absurdity by drawing out his internal qi.

Crack!

Chung Myung’s knee, which had converted all flexibility from his body into an offensive attack, mercilessly slammed into the wrist of the Black Dragon King, who was holding down the blade.

“Kuak...”

The first suppressed groan broke through the lips of the Black Dragon King.

At the very moment when the qi he had pulled up was about to flow into the blade, Chung Myung aimed his knee right at the wrist to block the path of the qi flow.

Kwang! Kwang! Kwang!

The knee, which flew in successively, continued to strike the wrist, which was already half split, and the bone exploded.

“Kuak!”

The Black Dragon King finally let out a single scream and flew back.

However, the one he was dealing with was a demon who never knew how to let go of his enemy.

Crack!

Chung Myung’s sword spun around and entangled the retreating Black Dragon King’s blade. Chung Myung, who charged in response, spun around, showing his blood-soaked teeth.

Paaat!

The sword qi, which was shot out from close range, sliced the Black Dragon King’s ankle bone. Before he could groan at the pain that came from his foot, another sword qi flew in from under his body and slashed at his face.

Slash!

Like a sharp blade peeling off the skin of a fruit, the sword qi flew into his face and cut from his chin to his eyes.

In an instant, one side of the Black Dragon King’s vision turned blood red.

“Weikkk!”

The Black Dragon King, who was shouting in a way that was hard to tell whether it was a shout or a scream, swung his blade down with terrifying force.

At that moment, five plum blossoms bloomed right in front of his blade.

Crack!

The fiercely swung blade literally crushed the insignificant flower, but the brief gap created in the process allowed Chung Myung’s body to pass him by.

Slash!

The eerie sensation of the side of his ankle being cut spread through him.

The Black Dragon King somehow managed to keep his body from swaying and turning. At the same time, he spun around and slashed his blade with all his might toward Chung Myung, who was moving away.

“This fucking dogggg!”

KWAAANGGGGG!

The explosive blade qi poured down like a raging storm toward Chung Myung’s back. Anyone familiar with martial arts would be shocked at the sheer power of the attack. But at that moment, Chung Myung turned around and raised his sword.

“AHHHH!”

A powerful downward strike continued from his upper body.

As a clear red line was drawn in the empty space where nothing existed, the blade qi split to the left and right like a thousand-year-old tree being cut down.

Plum blossoms bloomed one after another from the tip of Chung Myung’s sword, which descended downward. They wrapped around the Dark Fragrant Plum Blossom Sword and soon rose up.

At that moment, the Black Dragon King saw it.

The sight of the Plum Blossom qi, which had swelled like a wave above the blade qi, poured down toward him like a waterfall.

Plum Blossom Sword Form, Plum Blossom Flowing Like a River.

Kwaang!

The overflowing enhanced qi covered the blade qi, pushed it, and continued to move, flowing onward.

The river of red petals surged toward the Black Dragon King like a stream swollen by heavy rain.

“BRATTTTT!”

The Black Dragon King bit his lips. A crackling sound echoed in his ears, and blood splashed from his torn lips, but he felt no pain.

His bloodshot eyes drew out all his remaining qi.

Kwang!

He stepped forward and swung the blade, advancing ahead. The river of plum blossoms that poured in crashed into his blade, shattering, crumbling, and bouncing back.

“Woahhhh!”

Kwang!

The Black Dragon King took another step.

Swirling black qi broke through the red qi and advanced toward Chung Myung.

“Woaaah!”

He squeezed out all his strength.

The Black Dragon King, who had spent his entire life in the world of competition, had an intuition at that moment. The one who got pushed there would definitely be the one to die. It was a match where strength met strength, and internal qi clashed with internal qi. It was a spar where he could never afford to be pushed back.

His vision had been hazy for a long time.

The sea of petals around him, the storm of blade qi he had created, had already obscured his sight. Moreover, with one of his eyes cut, he could only see half the vision, unable to perceive beyond the clash of qi.

But he didn’t really need to see. His enemy would be at the end of the river of petals! The moment he took his last step and severed his opponent’s neck that would be his victory!

Puaah!

Blood gushed out from the Black Dragon King’s mouth and nose. The qi he had gathered and pulled up circulated explosively through his body and halted at the tip of his blade.

“AHHHHH!”

The Black Dragon King’s blade accelerated. Faster, stronger! His blade, which had become a storm that would swallow everything, literally slammed down and exploded the pouring plum blossom sword qi.

‘More!’

Just a little more!

His mind gradually emptied. Despite the pain of his dantian being pierced by the sharp blade, the Black Dragon King firmly stepped forward.

And at that moment, the force of the pouring plum blossom sword qi suddenly loosened.

‘Now!’

The Black Dragon King squeezed out all his qi and charged ahead. No, he tried to do so.

Paat!

At that moment, Chung Myung jumped out from the plum blossoms.

It was a surprise that the Black Dragon King would never have missed. But for the first time, he had to fight with just one eye instead of two, and he noticed Chung Myung’s presence a little later than usual.

‘No use though!’

The Black Dragon King swung his blade with all his might.

It didn’t matter if it was a little late! When that guy stabbed the sword into his body, his blade would split the guy’s waist...

But then.

Paaat!

All the muscles in his feet snapped.

The foot, which Chung Myung had cut a moment ago, couldn’t withstand the force of the Black Dragon King.

His body, which had been swinging the qi fiercely, staggered for a moment.

And!

Kwaang!

Chung Myung swung his sword with all his might and struck down the blade of the Black Dragon King. At that moment, the Black Dragon King heard it clearly—the eerie sound of his wrist breaking after enduring so much.

‘You?’

A cut eye.

A severed foot.

A persistently aimed wrist.

Chung Myung, who took advantage of the shock from the clash with the sword, rushed forward instead.

The moment he met those cold, cold eyes, the Black Dragon King realized it.

‘You were aiming for this from the start...?’

Paaat!

The sunset rainbow weapon, which seemed like it would cut down everything in the world, poured down towards the neck of the Black Dragon King.

Slash!

Chapter 978: There Is No Way I Wouldn't Believe. (3)

Flutter.

Something that rose into the air spun around violently. Everyone’s eyes on the island were glued to it.

The object, which had spun without pause and splashed blood in all directions, soon lost its strength and crashed.

Thud!

Everyone stared blankly at the sight.

An arm.

A warrior’s arm, trained to the max, crashed into the sand mound. The blood flowing from the cut dyed the white sand red.

No one could even breathe deeply.

It was hard to understand what they were seeing, even with their own eyes. The silence was so thick that even the sound of someone gulping felt like thunder.

Put!

At that moment, the Black Dragon King staggered backward with an eerie sound.

“Ugh....”

The Black Dragon King, who had lost his arm, grabbed his shoulder with his left hand. Blood was spurting out from between his thick fingers.

“Kuak....”

Everyone stared blankly at the sight.

The Black Dragon King’s right arm.

The arm that had commanded the Yangtze River while wielding that giant crescent blade was cut off just below the shoulder. When Chung Myung’s sword moved toward his neck, he reflexively raised his arm to prevent his head from being cut off, but the arm was severed without a trace.

Disbelief, shock. And fear.

With eyes filled with uncontrollable emotions, he stepped back and back.

“T-this...”

Everyone lost their minds as they looked at the Black Dragon King’s condition.

Who would have imagined it?

The sight of the arm of their leader, who reigned like a king on the Yangtze River and was now the leader of the Four Sects Alliance, being cut off by the sword of a third-class disciple of Mount Hua.

However, no matter how many times they closed their eyes and opened them, the sight didn’t change. The moment they realized that all of this was reality, a terrible sense of discomfort began to creep up on everyone on Plum Blossom Island.

Except for one group.

“As expected!”

“Damn it!”
