That couldn’t be. No matter how strong Mount Hua became, weren’t they the Nine Great Sects? They couldn’t be much weaker than Mount Hua.

But how should they interpret the situation where Mount Hua defeated the Evil Sects and rescued the Namgung family without others even lifting a finger?

No matter how much they thought about it, it was too unnatural.

They only suspected because of what had happened around Mount Hua so far, but if another sect did something similar, they would have already concluded there was a conspiracy on the Yangtze River.

There was no blood on Tang Gunak’s face.

‘Damn it.’

They probably didn’t understand. The obvious timing was difficult in their eyes.

The abbot was now cleverly provoking the pride of those watching the Yangtze River.

Was it a Three-Way Battle?

A minority’s argument doesn’t gain strength. But if this many people present had doubts, their voices would surely spread with power.

‘What should we do?’

How can we wisely overcome a situation where the most authoritative person in the world is determined to drive us out, and the most trusted people look at us with suspicion?

Even Tang Gunak, the greatest in the world, couldn’t speak rashly.

“Sect leader.”

At that moment, the abbot looked at Hyun Jong with a strange smile as if to drive in the last wedge.

“I will ask one last time. Did Mount Hua really make no agreement with the Evil Sects?”

Hyun Jong looked up at the sky, feeling lost.

‘It’s scary.’

It was so intense and terrifying. He thought he already knew Kangho, but this horrible malice made him shiver again.

“I will say it again!”

Just as Hyun Jong was about to raise his voice with force,

“...So what if we colluded?”

A cold voice pierced the air.

Everyone on the riverbanks turned toward the source.

Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, Chung Myung, was slowly walking toward them, his expression colder than the winds of the North Sea.

Chapter 985: This Is Why The World Is So Interesting. (5)

Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, Chung Myung.

That name certainly resonated within Kangho.

Whether advocating for Justice or Evil, wasn’t it the name of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint that everyone in Kangho couldn’t help but know?

But even for those familiar with that name and the symbolism Mount Hua’s Sword Saint held in the murim world, this was clearly an awkward sight.

Shaolin, Mount Hua, Sichuan Tang family.

The leaders of these three sects—none holding reputations as being fierce—were fiercely fighting against each other in a suffocating situation. The name of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint still seemed too small to be involved in such a conflict.

Furthermore, the rude words and actions he displayed, which were hardly worthy of being spoken, wouldn’t have been odd if he had been punished for disrupting talks between sects.

But Mount Hua said nothing to Chung Myung.

It was odd, yet perhaps understandable. Among those present, few could boast that they truly knew what kind of sect Mount Hua really was.

That left those watching with another real question.

‘Why isn’t the Tang family saying anything?’

It was the Tang family’s nature. Tang Gunak was explosively expressing his anger, and a third-class disciple had intervened in such an urgent situation. Wasn’t this enough to insult the Tang family?

Even though Mount Hua’s Sword Saint was close to the Sichuan Tang family, considering the unity of the Tang family—difficult for any sect in the world to match—it was not strange for Tang family members to harden their faces and attack Chung Myung. No, it was natural for them to do so.

But the Tang family was silent. They simply stared at Chung Myung, who stepped forward with hardened eyes. Even his gaze was filled with utmost trust.

It was natural for those watching to feel embarrassed.

‘It couldn’t be otherwise.’

The Gentle Crow Beggar slowly nodded after seeing the flustered faces of the Nine Great Sects.

He fully understood their bewilderment. If he had not witnessed with his own eyes what kind of person Mount Hua’s Sword Saint was at Plum Blossom Island, he would have stood there with the same expression.

In other words, if they had seen Mount Hua’s Sword Saint defeat the Black Dragon King at Plum Blossom Island firsthand, their reactions would have been completely different.

Chung Myung proved with his own hands that he was worthy of this conversation. The ranking was of utmost importance in the sect, but sometimes something else mattered more.

That was skill.

Who would dare say that someone who single-handedly took on and defeated the Black Dragon King was arrogant?

That was why the Gentle Crow Beggar could understand the reactions of both the Heavenly Friends Alliance, who looked at him with firm eyes, and the Nine Great Sects, who regarded him with suspicion.

Chung Myung, walking between the Heavenly Friends Alliance, opened his mouth after a long silence. His eyes stared straight at the abbot.

“What if we colluded?”

The abbot’s face, looking at Chung Myung, turned stern.

“What will you do?”

That sight was very symbolic.

The abbot was the abbot of Shaolin. It wouldn’t be an exaggeration to say he currently holds the highest status in Kangho.

The sight of a mere disciple of Mount Hua lashing out at the abbot of Shaolin made onlookers feel an extreme sense of alienation.

The abbot’s status, which had been met with gazes full of suspicion and disappointment, never collapsed. That status might have been invaded by a person who came in with mud on his feet.

“... Taoist.”

The abbot’s face darkened for a moment. However, he couldn’t show anger.

The man, Chung Myung, he knew was frivolous, but at the same time, every action had a hidden intention. He should never act according to Chung Myung’s wishes.

“That sounds very dangerous.”

That was why the abbot laughed instead—to show his affectionate side, embracing even a child as if he were a naked babe.

“Then, does the Taoist now admit that the Heavenly Friends Alliance and Evil Sect are in collusion?”

Chung Myung smiled.

“I told you.”

“...”

“So what are you going to do?”

The smile vanished quickly, and anger spread over the abbot’s face.

“You child!”

A voice loud enough for everyone to hear rang out.

“Don’t you know how important that is? Are you saying that?”

A stern reprimand. It was a tone showing superiority and making clear that the attitude of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint was not right for dealing with the Shaolin abbot.

No matter how right a story was, if it was not accompanied by appropriate attitude and manners, its light would fade, right?

But some people in the world do not attach importance to such things.

“Is it important?”

Chung Myung sneered.

“Right, it must be very important. To you.”

“See. Taoist...”

“But.”

Chung Myung’s cold voice cut off the abbot’s words.

“At least to us, none of that matters. The important thing was how to save the Namgung family.”

The abbot’s lips twitched slightly at the growling tone of words that sounded like a ferocious beast.

“While you were just watching those dying here, we jumped into the river ourselves and risked our lives to save Namgung. But what? Collude?”

Chung Myung’s eyes, filled with killing intent, turned to the abbot—no, more precisely towards the disciples hiding behind the abbot, trying to breathe slowly. They did not dare meet Chung Myung’s eyes and lowered their heads.

It wasn’t because they were afraid of his strength.

Those who worshipped justice and compromise could not deny that.

“I will ask again.”

“...”

“If we colluded, what are you going to do? Will you draw your sword and cut off all our throats?”

“Taoist, what I mean...”

“Try it.”

Chung Myung’s eyes, glaring at the abbot, dripped with killing intent.

“Do it.”

He bared his teeth like an animal, and the abbot clenched his lips at the force being projected.

In fact, when he pressed them, he didn’t have any plans for the aftermath. He just thought that if he pointed this out, he could reduce the criticism the Nine Great Sects would receive.

But when those who should have been protesting and making a fuss came out like this, he was speechless.

It was just as Chung Myung said.

If they had colluded with the Four Precepts Alliance, what would he do? Would he lead the Nine Great Sects and punish them?

At that moment, the abbot realized that the Heavenly Friends Alliance, whom they had never thought would be their opponent, had now grown to the point where they could stab them in the jaw.

Whether it was because the Nine Great Sects were in a state of self-restraint and had lost power or because the Heavenly Friends Alliance had grown beyond expectations and gained strength, the reason wasn’t so important.

What was important was that the Heavenly Friends Alliance was no longer afraid to fight them.

Look at this sight.

Even though Chung Myung was not hiding his killing intent and openly revealed it, Hyun Jong and Tang Gunak didn’t try to stop him. Didn’t this mean that even these two people with the most authority in the Heavenly Friends Alliance were not afraid to display hostility to the Nine Great Sects?

Not just that, the Heavenly Friends Alliance lined up behind him also glared with eyes that didn’t back down an inch.

When had he ever faced such hostility?

For the abbot, who had always been met with eyes full of admiration and trust, the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s hostility was too difficult to handle.

“Taoist.”

“It is disgusting. That ‘principle’ you keep talking about.”

There was no warmth in Chung Myung’s gaze.

The abbot probably wouldn’t think he was wrong. He started by trying to hold the Heavenly Friends Alliance back, but as he talked, he probably came to believe his logic was right.

He felt even more upset knowing that.

The shamelessness of not feeling an ounce of shame while talking about how their nobility was more important than those who were dying, the cruelty of turning a blind eye to those being stabbed to death while talking about great principles and values.

How many people had died under that logic?

Do not join hands with the Evil Sect. You cannot negotiate with those barbarians outside the land. A warrior must not defile himself even if he dies. How much blood has been shed on the ground by the logic of those high-ranking men who never shed blood on the battlefield but only talk from the rear?

What Chung Myung really couldn’t stand was that they didn’t take any responsibility for the sacrifice.

Collusion? Maybe. In their eyes, the agreement between Hyun Jong and Jang Ilso might seem like it.

Of course, they could refute it as much as they wanted, but there was no reason to do so.

Even if not now, if needed, they would collude whenever they wanted.

If it could save even one more disciple of Mount Hua, Chung Myung wouldn’t hesitate to lick the feet of Jang Ilso. Even if the world pointed a finger at him, he could laugh at them.

So, there was no reason to get angry over such a ridiculous thing.

“Say whatever you want to.”

“...”

“No matter what we say, it’s going to be like that anyway, right?”

Chung Myung’s lips curled into a snicker. That blatant insult pierced the abbot’s heart.

“So go ahead and say whatever you want. That we are colluding with the Evil Sect to take over Central Plains. That everything that happened on Plum Blossom Island was the work of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

Chung Myung glanced at the abbot and the disciples of the Nine Great Sects.

“Isn’t that your way?”

“Taoist! Is that something that comes out of the mouth of someone who worships righteousness?”

The abbot raised his voice as if he couldn’t listen any longer. However, even after hearing that scolding, Chung Myung’s face remained cold.

“Righteousness?”

Chung Myung sneered, twisting the corners of his lips.

“I don’t know about that.”

“... What did you say?”

“I don’t know about that. Righteousness, whatever... You guys can say whatever you want. I’m just doing what I have to do.”

Chung Myung coldly rebuked the abbot, who was momentarily speechless.

“Do you know how to avoid committing a crime?”

The abbot’s eyes filled with doubt at the unexpected question. When Chung Myung answered, he simply shut his eyes tightly.

“It’s to not do anything. Just like you guys who were here.”

A calm silence flowed along the river.

There was no need to judge right or wrong.

Even just looking at the disciples of the Nine Great Sects who couldn’t bear to look straight ahead and the followers of the Heavenly Friends Alliance who proudly raised their heads, it was clear who was right.

“One day, you might find yourself in the same situation as Namgung.”

“...”

“Say the same thing then. You have to do it by the rules. If you nod your head, saying that this is right even when your neck is to be cut off, I will at least acknowledge that belief.”

The bodies of the Nine Great Sect disciples trembled slightly.

“But if you cannot do that...”

The killing intent flowing from Chung Myung stabbed everyone’s hearts like a knife.

“Shut that disgusting mouth and get out. I cannot stand you anymore.”

“Taoist!”

The moment the abbot finally moved, Chung Myung glanced at him.

“....”

The abbot’s shoulders, which had been tense, sank as his gaze turned cold.

“You should have killed me three years ago.”

“You...”

“Right?”

Chung Myung smiled brightly and turned around without any regrets.

Chapter 986: We Saw Nothing. (1)

The abbot’s fingers trembled slightly.

“That...”

Watching Chung Myung turn away after speaking his mind, the abbot felt as if he had swallowed a hot coal.

But there was nothing he could do. He knew all too well how futile it would be to argue with Chung Myung once he had already turned his back.

“Mount Hua’s Sword Saint!”

The atmosphere shifted completely. Now, no one dared to challenge the words of Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.

No matter how loudly the abbot shouted, here, his words carried no weight.

So, the abbot redirected his arrows at someone else, not Chung Myung.

“Is... is this what the Heavenly Friends Alliance stands for?”

He tried to steady his voice and glared directly at Hyun Jong and Tang Gunak.

No matter how much authority Mount Hua’s Sword Saint’s words held, he was not officially appointed within the Alliance. That meant his words could not represent the will of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

Ultimately, the will of the Heavenly Friends Alliance was determined by Hyun Jong and Tang Gunak.

The abbot pressed them with his gaze.

“Answer me. Is this truly the will of the Heavenly Friends Alliance?”

Hyun Jong met his gaze calmly.

“It’s a bit much to call it the will of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, but I’m not certain...”

The abbot’s face twisted at the vague response.

“Sect leader!”

He tried to press him urgently, but Hyun Jong simply shook his head slowly.

“As that child said, even if given another chance, we would head to Plum Blossom Island without hesitation.”

“...”

“There’s not a shred of shame in what we did. If the world criticizes us, wouldn’t that be inevitable? From the start, everything we did wasn’t meant to be shown to the world, so we have no choice but to accept it.”

The abbot stared at Hyun Jong, eyes wide with disbelief.

What did all this mean?

Wasn’t this whole scandal supposed to be revealed to the world? Would they accept the criticism?

Could these words really come from someone who led a sect or an alliance?

“Abbot.”

Hyun Jong sighed.

“Nothing in this world is perfect.”

“...”

“That’s why nothing is completely right. So I can only ask myself: Am I truly on the right path?”

This probably wasn’t meant as a criticism of the abbot, but the abbot felt as if Hyun Jong was silently asking, “Do you really believe you are on the right side?” A deep discomfort gnawed at him.

Yet Hyun Jong’s eyes did not waver. In their depths lay his unwavering belief in his convictions.

“My heart tells me I did the right thing. So what does it matter what others think? Please do not force Shaolin’s way upon us. The Heavenly Friends Alliance will do what it believes is right.”

“...”

“I think that’s the answer. Then.”

Hyun Jong stepped back.

Of course, the abbot wanted to hold him there. He knew he couldn’t let them leave like this.

But he was powerless. Someone approached from behind Hyun Jong.

A figure whose once white robe was stained crimson with blood. A person limping forward steps heavy with sincere resentment.

No matter how urgent the abbot’s thoughts, he couldn’t ignore this person and stop Hyun Jong.

That person was none other than Namgung Do-Wei.

“... Disciple Namgung.”

Namgung Do-Wei stood before the abbot, eyes burning with resentment.

Why wouldn’t he be?

Shaolin had utterly betrayed Namgung’s hopes.

It would have been better if he had never appeared by the riverside, but to come all this way and watch Namgung be thoroughly trampled was unforgivable.

Of course, the abbot had his reasons, but would they matter to the Namgung family, who had endured such hell?

Even the abbot never thought he could be forgiven by Namgung. Yet when he faced Namgung Do-Wei—the Namgung family member who harbored the deepest grudge against Shaolin—he was left speechless.

While the abbot hesitated, Namgung Do-Wei spoke first.

“... Collusion.”

A hollow laugh escaped him.

“If what the abbot says is true, then our Namgung family saved their lives in exchange for collusion between the Four Precepts Alliance and the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“D-disciple.”

“What should we do?”

A murderous glare suddenly flared in Namgung Do-Wei’s sunken eyes.

“This pitiful life saved by their dirty collusion—should I commit suicide now and reclaim your honor?”

“Disciple Namgung. What I mean is...”

“Haha... Hahahaha.”

Namgung Do-Wei let out a twisted laugh.

“Those who should have died and fallen have come back to life for no reason and upset the abbot. I apologize for that.”

“That is not what I meant.”

“Really?”

Namgung Do-Wei’s serious expression, as if he had never laughed, pressed the abbot.

His eyes asked:

Did Shaolin truly want the Namgung family to return alive from that island? From the moment he gave up on rescuing them, didn’t he wish there were no survivors?

The abbot could not answer.

If Namgung Do-Wei had been the only problem, he could have suppressed him with his authority. But when all the Namgung survivors who had regained their senses looked at him as their mortal enemy, what could he say?

Even if he had ten mouths, he would have nothing to say.

“If you don’t want to see me, then it’s right for us to disappear.”

Namgung Do-Wei slowly looked around at everyone.

“As the young lord of the Namgung family... and acting lord with the official lord no longer present!”

His voice rang out majestically.

“From now on, the Namgung family will withdraw from the Five Great Families and pledge our friendship to the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“N-Namgung disciple!”

A voice close to a scream escaped the abbot.

He had thought Namgung Do-Wei would naturally resent the Nine Great Sects, but how could he have known he would make such a bold declaration right then?

“This is not something to be decided emotionally!”

“Right, you should be rational. Rational like the abbot who thoroughly watched over Plum Blossom Island from here!”

“...”

Namgung Do-Wei bared his teeth and spat those words at the speechless abbot.

“So when you claim that the Heavenly Friends Alliance is in collusion with the Evil Sect, please include this as well: the Namgung family has also become lackeys of those Evil Sect bastards.”

“...”

“Please do that, abbot.”

Namgung Do-Wei turned away as if unwilling to speak further.

The abbot’s face went pale.

‘T-this cannot be happening.’

The Namgung family alone could not be sent to the Heavenly Friends Alliance. No, even if they were to join, it couldn’t be now!

Who would believe this?

The moment the Namgung family joined the Heavenly Friends Alliance, everything the abbot had said would be rendered meaningless. Even his suspicions about the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s intentions would become a scandal!

All his justifications would benefit the Heavenly Friends Alliance, and the status of Shaolin and the Nine Great Sects would be utterly destroyed.

That must be stopped. That one thing!

“Disciple! Think again! Namgung family...”

“Enough!”

At that moment, someone grabbed the abbot’s shoulder.

Turning around, he saw Jong Ri-Hyung approaching, his face contorted with concern.

“This is not the time, abbot.”

“... Sect leader.”

“It’s not wrong to raise suspicion. But there is a time and place, isn’t there? Injured people are groaning over there. Suppose the abbot, who speaks of the Buddha’s mercy, prioritizes revealing the truth and saving Shaolin’s reputation over treating the wounded. How would the world see him?”

“You’re being too much!”

The abbot shouted, his face flushed red.

Jong Ri-Hyung bit his lip tightly. He wanted to say harsher words but held back.

No matter how distasteful, Shaolin was the leader of the Nine Great Sects.

Kongtong was one of the Nine Great Sects, and among them, Shaolin was the only sect worth siding with.

Whether he liked it or not, he had to maintain relations with Shaolin, so he couldn’t press further.

“... Just let it go, for now, abbot.”

Lowering his voice, he added,

“Aren’t there too many eyes watching?”

At those words, the abbot instinctively turned around. The beggars of the Beggars Union, standing behind Shaolin and Kongtong, watched the scene with bright eyes.

Their job was to prevent rumors from spreading worldwide. No matter how much the Beggars Union controlled information, they couldn’t block all eyes and mouths.

And no matter how he looked at them, their eyes were not favorable to him. If he pushed harder, who knew what words might spread?

‘Damn it.’

The abbot bit his lip. His fist, hidden beneath his long sleeve, trembled.

Now, he had no choice but to admit it: the mistake he made at the river was too great. But even so, wasn’t the price too severe and harsh?

The abbot felt like beating his chest in frustration.

They had rushed all this way to save Namgung. How could he not know that Shaolin also had good intentions toward the Namgung family?

“Then do handle things, abbot.”

Hyun Jong bowed deeply toward the abbot.

That extreme courtesy seemed to show his determination not to be caught even a little until the moment of departure.

He soon straightened and, without waiting for the abbot to respond, turned and shouted,

“Find a place to stay and move the injured there!”

“Yes, sect leader!”

The disciples of the Nine Great Sects and the followers of the Heavenly Friends Alliance began moving busily according to Hyun Jong’s orders. Tang Gunak, who had stayed until the end, also turned away after casting the abbot a cold glance.

The abbot stared straight ahead, eyes red, his body trembling from extreme humiliation.

The Heavenly Friends Alliance, perfectly aligned, moved away from them in an orderly fashion.

‘In the end...’

Blood trickled down his clenched lips.

‘Is this how it will end?’

The abbot did not want to admit it, but at this point, he had no choice.

‘I cannot embrace them.’

Even when he had friction with Mount Hua, he had been furious, but deep down, he believed he would lead the Heavenly Friends Alliance back onto the right path. The conflict, born of a temporary difference of opinion, would naturally heal over time.

But now, he knew for certain.

They were not the ones who could stand with the Justice faction.

“... In the end, it is just as you said.”

“Uh?”

“No. It’s nothing.”

The abbot’s eyes held a coldness, unlike his usual self.

Today, at this very moment,

An irreparable rift had formed between the Nine Great Sects and the Heavenly Friends Alliance—the two great forces representing justice in Kangho.

No one knew where this divide, born in chaotic times when the Evil Sect ran rampant, would lead Kangho.

Chapter 987: We Saw Nothing. (2)

Although they left the riverside with great momentum, Mount Hua and the Tang family didn’t actually move very far. They needed to find a nearby place to stay to care for the seriously injured Namgung family, and the village they had built at the river’s mouth was perfect for Mount Hua and the Tang family to live in.

“But isn’t it too close to the Nine Sects? Won’t that be uncomfortable?”

“If it’s uncomfortable, it’s the guys standing there with their legs stuck who need to feel it. Why should we?”

“That makes sense.”

So Mount Hua settled in a manner that Green Forest had used in the past. After tending to the injured without even having time to unpack, they managed to regain some stability by sunset.

Of course, the injured—not Mount Hua.

“The more I think about it, the angrier I get. That crazy bald head!”

Chung Myung’s eyes shone with fury.

“What? Collude? Colludeeee? That insane bastard is really talking about collusion? I’ll slam his wooden beads right into his mouth! Where does he think he’s going with that slander?”

“Ahem, Chung Myung.”

“What!”

Baek Cheon coughed softly. He understood the anger but seemed displeased with how harsh Chung Myung was being toward the abbot of Shaolin.

“You’re speaking too harshly about a reputable person in Kangho.”

“Are you insane?”

“That’s what I meant... It could be an illness. Like dementia.”

“...”

Jo Gul looked puzzled and asked Yoon, who was beside him,

“Sahyung, does dementia affect warriors too?”

“I thought it wouldn’t.”

“But him?”

“After seeing what happened today, I think it can.”

“Ah...”

It was a normal conversation any disciple of Mount Hua would be familiar with. The only minor problem was that, this time, there was a bald man with a shiny head right in front of them.

Hae Yeon, who couldn’t bring himself to join the conversation, was sweating profusely. As he rolled his eyes anxiously, sparks seemed to fly from them.

“Yah, what do you think, Baldie?”

“A-amitabha... w-what are you talking about?”

“The abbot bastard seems to have gone fully insane. What do you think about it?”

Hae Yeon shut his eyes tightly. Since he had eyes, he couldn’t help but witness everything that happened between Mount Hua and Shaolin. When the abbot accused them of colluding with the Evil Sect, didn’t he almost curse at the abbot without realizing it?

If that had actually happened, it would have been a moment of great destruction, one that would be remembered in Shaolin’s history.

“Ah, what do I think?”

Hae Yeon was overcome with agony. Even though he was no longer a member of Shaolin, the abbot was like a father to him. But he couldn’t just gossip about such a person in front of others!

Of course, Chung Myung didn’t give Hae Yeon even a little consideration. Instead of thinking about it, he tilted his head crookedly and thrust his face in front of Hae Yeon.

“Are you deaf? Your head is so shiny; are your ears the same?”

Jo Gul whispered to Yoon Jong,

“Sahyung, don’t you hear better when your ears are shiny and clean, though?”

“... I’m no physician, so I don’t know...”

Hae Yeon furtively looked at the two and bit his lip. He hated those guys even more.

“Why aren’t you answering?”

At that moment, Baek Cheon, who had been silently watching Chung Myung talk, frowned.

“Chung Myung.”

“Ah, what now!”

“Monk Hae Yeon is having trouble answering. Stop asking him now.”

Chung Myung frowned. But before he could swear, Baek Cheon calmly said,

“Some people are like that. Your body is with Mount Hua, but your heart is with Shaolin. How can we stop that? Only by understanding that conflict can we be called true friends.”

“Ah, really? So you like Shaolin even now?”

Hae Yeon changed his mind. Baek Cheon, that bastard... no, he hated Taoist Baek Cheon even more.

“Ahhh. I was raising a tiger cub. So, you’ll still side with the guy who said we were colluding with the Evil Sect? If we get to know him now, isn’t he like a spy who conspired with the abbot and infiltrated Mount Hua?”

“Hmm?”

“Uh?”

The Five Swords all focused their eyes on one place. Hae Yeon, who had been blinking, jumped up.

“S-spy! How can a Buddhist even do such a thing?”

“Why aren’t you speaking, you bastard! Your abbot is clearly selling out the nation! Do you still stay silent after seeing that? Isn’t it normal for Shaolin people to be born with a guilty conscience?”

“T-that is...”

Cold sweat dripped down Hae Yeon’s head.

“So, did you see what your abbot did earlier or not?”

“I-I....”

“Right, you.”

“I-I did not see anything.”

“...”

At that moment, everyone stared blankly at Hae Yeon. He closed his eyes tightly.

“Really? You didn’t?”

Chung Myung got up from his place, made a pleased look, and then hit Hae Yeon on the head right away.

“Kuaaak!”

“Catch him!”

“I knew this would happen!”

The frightened Five Swords grabbed Chung Myung as he rushed at Hae Yeon.

“Let go! Will you not let go? That little bastard, shameless! That’s why one cannot take in such black-haired beasts! What? You didn’t see? You little brat! Those eyes are useless then; why not pluck them out today?”

“Chung Myung! Calm down!”

“Brat, are you really going to beat people up because of that?”

“Right, Chung Myung! I don’t know about other things, but monk Hae Yeon is no black-haired beast! He doesn’t even have hair!”

“...”

“...”

A moment of silence fell. Chung Myung, who was rushing in, and the Five Swords, who were holding him back, both looked blankly at Jo Gul.

“... why?”

“... fuck.”

“Amazing.”

“Taoist, that is a bit...”

“Shut up, you bastard!”

Chung Myung ran toward Hae Yeon once again.

This kind of thing had become a daily routine for Mount Hua, so no one was paying attention to that place—except for one.

“...”

Namgung Do-Wei stared blankly as Chung Myung bit Hae Yeon’s head with his teeth.

‘What is that?’

Was that the person he saw?

He stared intently at the scene and grabbed Tang Pae, who was passing by.

“E-excuse me, young lord.”

“Uh?”

“... is that how they normally are?”

Tang Pae raised his head slightly at the question and looked at Chung Myung and the Five Swords. Then he answered calmly,

“Well... you’ll get used to it soon enough.”

Then he patted Namgung Do-Wei’s shoulder a couple of times as if feeling sorry for him and went his own way.

Now Chung Myung was completely beating Hae Yeon up. Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes fluttered at the unbelievable sight.

‘Come to think of it...’

It seemed like he was originally like that. Right, the Worldly Murim Tournament. The tournament... right, at the tournament.

‘But even so, this is!’

Wasn’t that the warrior who showed heroic deeds by defeating the Black Dragon King at Plum Blossom Island yesterday? The sword he wielded still lingered in Namgung Do-Wei’s mind, causing him to shiver.

But this guy was...

“Die, die! You bat-like bastard!”

“N-not a bat!”

“Not a bat? Where is this bald guy trying to decide? Grow some hair now! Take off the clothes! Do one of the two!”

“T-that...”

He was doing it. Ah, he was doing it.

In his head, he knew they were the same person, but his heart couldn’t accept it.

“Ah, calm down, brat! Why are you venting your anger on Shaolin at the monk... uh?”

At that moment, Yoon Jong noticed Namgung Do-Wei standing blankly.

“Ah, hello?”

“... Ah, hello... Taoist.”

Yoon Jong greeted him politely, but Namgung Do-Wei’s expression upon receiving the greeting was completely cold.

“What? Isn’t that an injured person? Why is he crawling out now?”

When Chung Myung’s attention was drawn to him, Namgung Do-Wei quickly cleared his throat. Humans are odd, but he had to do what he had to do.

“Taoist, I came to formally thank you again.”

Namgung Do-Wei bowed deeply.

Chung Myung, who had seen that, blankly turned his gaze toward Baek Cheon and asked.

“Sasuk.”

“Uh?”

“Are sect people normally like that? Do they have to say stuff like that?”

“... why are you asking me that?”

“That’s because Sasuk’s core is with the Southern Edge sect, right?”

“I said it isn’t that, you bastard! I am a man of Mount Hua to the core!”

“S-see such a total rotten brat. Tsk tsk, betraying the parents who gave you the good name of Dong-Ryong.”

“I don’t want to hear that from you!”

Ah, right. Come to think of it, that guy was Mount Hua’s Sword Saint, who fought against the pirates of the Yangtze River. Back then... he was so cool and dependable.

‘But his name is Dong-Ryong?’

A person’s name didn’t necessarily have to match their face, but at this point, wasn’t it so dissonant that they were fighting each other by the collar?

“Right. Well, being polite isn’t a bad thing.”

Chung Myung nodded dryly. Contrary to his words, his eyes clearly showed a look that said, ‘Where can I use that useless brat?’ It was obvious to Namgung Do-Wei.

“So, done with your deed?”

“Ah, no.”

Namgung Do-Wei kept wiping the sweat dripping down his face.

“Ah, since we’re going to be one unit, I came to tell you that I’m requesting help from you.”

Namgung Do-Wei bowed deeply again.

“Please give me lots of guidance.”

He was deeply convinced.

‘Mount Hua has become even stronger.’

He knew all too well how strong Mount Hua was originally. Just a few years ago, Mount Hua was a sect with no name in the murim world.

‘And even during the tournament, it was just a sect with a good status.’

However, what they showed in the river this time completely shattered Namgung Do-Wei’s thoughts. In just a few years, they had become a sect with power on par with the Namgung family, no, maybe even greater.

Common sense would say it was nonsense, but there was no denying what he had seen with his own eyes.

‘In other words, the people who know the best way to rebuild the sect in the world right now are Mount Hua.’

So, no matter what happened, he had to stick close to them.

To perfectly rebuild the half-collapsed Namgung family, he had to learn from Mount Hua and Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.

‘Chung Myung Taoist will definitely help. Surely he will.’

Chung Myung risked his life to save people he had never met. If he thought about that deep sense of loyalty, he would never turn his back on the Namgung family.

Namgung Do-Wei thought so.

The problem was...

“Sasuk.”

“Uh?”

“What is he saying?”

“Now that they’ve joined the Heavenly Friends Alliance, they’re asking you to take good care of them.”

“So what is he asking?”

“He said in front of the Shaolin abbot that he was leaving the Five Great Families and joining the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Didn’t you hear?”

“Ah, that?”

Chung Myung nodded as if he finally understood.

“Yes! That’s it!”

Namgung Do-Wei stared at Chung Myung with a bright face.

“So from now...”

“Yah.”

“... Uh?”

Namgung Do-Wei, who was about to lower his head again, hesitated for a moment and then lifted it.

And then he saw Chung Myung, who was looking at him with his head tilted to the side as if poking all the world’s ugliness into him.

“Joined?”

“... Yes, joined...”

“Who is accepting, though?”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung poked his ear and blew his finger.

“You won’t join.”

“Uh?”

“I said you won’t join.”

“....”

Yes, the problem was that Chung Myung was a stranger than Namgung Do-Wei thought.

And it was sad.

Chapter 988: We Saw Nothing. (3)

“Taoist.”

“Go.”

“That... Taoist.”

“Ah, go.”

“Taoist...”

“Ah, shit!”

Chung Myung spun around and kicked Namgung Do-Wei.

Kwang!

“Kuak!”

Namgung Do-Wei rolled across the ground after the kick.

“This bastard! The more I let him, the more he drags me down!”

Chung Myung glared at Namgung Do-Wei with wide eyes.

“Yah! Do you think we’re that easy? Just because you tell us to accept, do we have to? The world’s best Namgung family is joining, so how can the beggars from the Heavenly Friends Alliance refuse?”

Namgung Do-Wei jumped up in shock.

“T-taoist! That’s not what I meant...”

“It’s not? It’s not, and what? Did the sect leader give you permission? Did the Tang lord say it was all right? Ohhh! Not that either? Now that you’re the Lord of the Namgung family, the sect leader of Mount Hua and the Tang family lord don’t matter to you?”

“M-me?”

I did that?

“Yes, you! You bastard! Is there another Namgung here?”

Namgung Do-Wei looked at Chung Myung as if he’d seen a ghost.

When in his life had he ever encountered such a person? As the Lord of the Namgung family, he had always lived with expectations and support, so the existence of a human named Chung Myung was too cruel.

However, as always, Chung Myung didn’t care about others’ positions.

“Who does this brat think he is? When he was doing well, he’d go around saying the Five Great Families were the leaders and rulers of Anhui! Now that he’s lost all his legs, what? Unity? Family? I’ve never even eaten with you, so who are you thinking of calling an alliance or family, you brat?”

“T-that isn’t it, Taoist! First, listen to what I have to say...”

“No need!”

The Five Swords watched this scene from a short distance with strange expressions. The Namgung family saw someone throw away all their dignity as the next Lord, clinging to Chung Myung, and Jo Gul looked back at Baek Cheon with an odd look.

“Um... Sasuk.”

“Uh?”

“Was that man always like this?”

“...”

‘How would I know?’ was all Baek Cheon could say.

What could he say? The man had looked clean and neat at the tournament...

Yoon Jong scratched his head, clearly confused.

“Why is he acting like that?”

Baek Cheon shrugged.

“From Namgung’s young lord’s perspective, he has no choice but to join the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“For sure?”

“Yes. There’s no other way.”

Baek Cheon looked quietly at Namgung Do-Wei.

‘Anhui is too dangerous now.’

Anhui, in Zhejiang province, bordered the Yangtze River. It didn’t fully overlap with the pirates’ direct territory, but it was a place where pirates could lead a fleet and attempt a landing whenever they wanted.

And...

‘The pirates must be enraged now.’

Of course, the pirates’ morale is at an all-time low, but the Black Dragon King’s eyes are likely wide open.

Why not?

Even if all of this was Jang Ilso’s plan, the pirates were defeated miserably by the Heavenly Friends Alliance, and the Black Dragon King lost one of his arms to Chung Myung.

The Black Dragon King would try to vent his anger somewhere. The Namgung family would be the perfect target.

This all started when the Namgung family attacked the pirates. And the Namgung family’s strength was now less than a third of its original number.

“The pirates will attack Namgung?”

When Yu Yiseol said this as if she had guessed, Baek Cheon nodded.

“Normally, the Namgung family wouldn’t be afraid even if the pirates attacked, but... the situation is not good now.”

They had lost too many forces on Plum Blossom Island, but the bigger problem was that they had turned their backs on the Nine Great Sects.

In this situation, even if the pirates attacked the Namgung family, would the Nine Sects help?

Baek Cheon scoffed quietly.

‘No, I don’t know. Even if they hadn’t turned their backs, would the Nine Sects really have helped them?’

Baek Cheon had seen enough to no longer trust the Nine Great Sects.

“The pirates’ defeat isn’t a big problem in the grand scheme. The problem is that the Black Dragon King has probably lost much control over the pirates because of this incident. Isn’t it a basic murim strategy to attack external enemies and control internal troubles?”

“Yes, Sasuk.”

Yoon Jong nodded as if he understood.

“From the pirates’ perspective, the Namgung family is the easiest prey right now.”

“Yes.”

At that moment, Jo Gul spoke.

“That’s not all.”

“Uh?”

Baek Cheon turned toward Jo Gul, who looked at Namgung Do-Wei with slightly bitter eyes as if he knew something.

“A family sect is based on strength. No matter how honest and pure they try to be, problems are inevitable.”

“...Past grudges can resurface, that’s what you mean.”

“Yes. Kangho isn’t a place that leaves weak spots alone.”

Tang Soso nodded bitterly.

“Sahyung is right, Sasuk.”

“Hmm.”

Baek Cheon sighed softly.

‘Right. No sect has felt this as deeply as Mount Hua.’

Just think about when Mount Hua lost their strength due to the Demonic Cult’s attack. Did those who tried to take everything from Mount Hua hold a special grudge?

‘No. That wouldn’t be the case.’

When the strong lost power, those who once greeted them with smiles turned into robbers. Kangho was a place where smiles could turn into theft. If this continued, even if the pirates fell silent, the Namgung family would likely suffer a fate similar to Mount Hua’s past.

“Since they can no longer be with the Nine Great Sects, no sect will help the Namgung family. The remaining Five Great Families are too far apart... and, as you know, they’re not on good terms.”

“Right.”

“So... the young Lord of Namgung needs a roof right now. Not for himself, but for the surviving members of the Namgung family to rest.”

“Hmm.”

Yoon Jong nodded in understanding.

“But, Sasuk.”

“What? Do you have more questions?”

“... It’s not something that needs an answer.”

“Then what?”

Yoon Jong looked at Namgung Do-Wei with a trembling face and said,

“If he wants to join the Heavenly Friends Alliance, he can just tell the sect leader or Tang lord. So why is he acting like that with Chung Myung? He must have already seen that guy can’t do anything when the sect leader orders it.”

“...”

Namgung Do-Wei was tirelessly clinging to Chung Myung, getting kicked and pushed back. Baek Cheon watched with an odd expression.

“...His injuries are too serious. He probably doesn’t have good judgment now.”

“...”

They couldn’t understand the situation at all.

In the past, many bandits were busy writing accounts, but quite a few people gathered and sat around the main room.

Namgung Do-Wei, who was able to join thanks to Hyun Jong’s consideration, gulped as he looked at the faces gathered.

The weight felt so different.

If he had been Namgung Do-Wei in the past, he might not have felt this pressure. But now, he had to desperately hold back from shrinking.

Did the Namgung family, having lost Namgung Hwang and standing at a crossroads, feel unworthy?

No.

‘It’s because their status is different now.’

It didn’t matter if he called himself weak. That was the truth.

Those who haven’t experienced it probably wouldn’t understand. But anyone in Namgung Do-Wei’s position would feel similar emotions.

Namgung Do-Wei glanced at Hyun Jong.

‘Even that person is the same.’

When he first saw Hyun Jong, he was the sect leader of a nameless sect. Although he sat at the high table with sect leaders from the tournament, it wouldn’t have been odd if Hyun Jong had been kicked out at any moment.

But when they met again on the Yangtze River, he had become the sect leader of the Great Mount Hua sect, which had defeated even Wudang and become one of Kangho’s core sects. Now, as leader of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, he could stand up to the Shaolin abbot and Jang Ilso of the Four Precepts Alliance.

What would be strange about being nervous in front of such a person? Rather, anyone not nervous would be insane.

‘It’s not just sect leader Hyun Jong.’

Tang Gunak, the Tang family lord sitting next to him, was the same.

Of course, Tang Gunak had a high status in the past.

But could Tang Gunak from a few years ago compare to the second-in-command of the Heavenly Friends Alliance now?

That was impossible.

Namgung Do-Wei knew the power of the Five Great Families best. Though they were equal in name, no matter how hard they tried, they could never compare to the Nine Great Sects. Even Namgung Hwang wouldn’t dare stand on par with the Shaolin abbot.

However, the Heavenly Friends Alliance was different. Hadn’t they already proven they could stand on par with the Nine Great Sects?

The disciples of Mount Hua and the Tang family guarding their sides were also extremely strong.

‘The problem is that this isn’t all.’

The power of the Heavenly Friends Alliance still remained. They could call in the Nanman Beast Palace and the North Sea Ice Palace at any time.

This was why he couldn’t help but realize just how great the Heavenly Friends Alliance was.

And decisively!

Namgung Do-Wei’s gaze turned to Chung Myung, whose face displayed pure annoyance.

‘A young swordsman who defeated the Black Dragon King.’

He didn’t know for sure, but the moment this fact spread throughout Kangho, there would be a great uproar. No, maybe the uproar had already begun, and the world had turned upside down.

Chung Myung has been famous until now. Many were called the best in the world, but Chung Myung alone became famous for one reason.

‘That person is not just a possibility.’

In other words, Chung Myung was someone who would definitely become the best in the world.

There was no best in the world in Kangho right now. Not just now, but most of the time, there was no definitive best.

In the end, you had to compete to find out who was strongest. But skilled warriors of a sect had too much at stake to easily compete.

If they lost, they had too much to lose.

That’s why there were those called the best in the world, but it’s difficult for a true best to exist. That’s why terms like the Three Greatest Swordsmen or the Five Great Swordsmen existed.

But Chung Myung was not among them.

He had made it clear at the tournament that he would one day become the best, and his subsequent actions showed that day wasn’t far off.

‘And the Black Dragon King.’

Now, everyone would realize it. The Heavenly Friends Alliance would soon have the undoubted best warriors in the world.

No one in Kangho didn’t understand what that meant.

‘The order of Kangho can truly change.’

Changed by Chung Myung’s hands!

Namgung Do-Wei clenched his fists. It was too much to follow, but he couldn’t afford not to. The path he was on led to the top.

‘So I must join the Heavenly Friends Alliance...’

Just as Namgung Do-Wei was about to make up his mind again,

“Huhuhuhuh. It’s nice to meet you all again like this. Well, it’s all thanks to me preparing the ship on time, but don’t thank me. It’s only natural for Heavenly Friends Alliance members to help each other... Kuak!”

Chung Myung’s fist suddenly landed on the chin of Im So-Byeong, who was acting cute.

“This punk, I was just going to let it go, but you keep scratching me? Yeah, bastard! If you’d come even a little earlier, I would’ve kicked Jang Ilso’s butt! I told you to hurry up and bring the ship, but you couldn’t even do that properly because you were late!”

Thinking about it, Chung Myung got angrier and climbed on top of Im So-Byeong’s stomach, swinging his fists. Im So-Byeong protested while desperately blocking the punches raining down on his face.

“N-no! Why is that my fault? I did my best...!”

“Ohh, this man is Evil Sect at the core, so you couldn’t stand to see the Evil Sect leader’s balls fly off? Stop making excuses and die now, you bastard! Die!”

“No, I’m that bastard’s enemy! Ackk! I’m going insane!”

Cold sweat ran down Namgung Do-Wei’s forehead.

‘W-what is...?’

How could something like this happen in a place where so many people were gathered?

“Ahem.”

At that moment, Hyun Jong cleared his throat softly.

Namgung Do-Wei turned to Hyun Jong with a look that said, ‘Then, of course, that deserves punishment...’

“Let’s start the meeting.”

“Yes.”

“Die! Die, you bastard!”

“First, let’s get to the first issue...”

Chung Myung, who was beating Im So-Byeong, and the others who were starting the discussion acted as if it was nothing.

Namgung Do-Wei, blankly watching the horrifying scene, looked up at the ceiling.

‘Should I just ask them to put me back into the Five Great Families?’

It certainly seemed... he wouldn’t adapt easily.

Ha... haha...

Chapter 989: We Saw Nothing. (4)

Namgung Do-Wei wasn’t the only stranger at the Heavenly Friends Alliance meeting.

“Can I stay here?”

The Gentle Crow Beggar scratched the back of his head, wearing an awkward expression.

The reason he had followed Namgung Do-Wei here was simple. Since he had declared his intention to leave on his own, he couldn’t return to the Beggars Union, and standing around outside didn’t look good.

So, he planned to hang around for a while, sneak out when he felt unnoticed, and find somewhere else to go.

That was until he received the red envelope that morning.

The envelope had come directly to him, containing only the brief message: “Stay here,” sealed proudly with the Beggars Union lord’s mark.

“Damn.”

Gentle Crow Beggar bit his lip.

“A gentleman lying in a hospital bed is quick to respond.”

Though he was no longer a member of the Beggars Union, he still couldn’t ignore the orders of its lord. It was customary for an elder to give up martial arts to leave the sect. He had to lie low now, even if it meant creating a situation where he could sneak back later.

Well, that was manageable, even if a bit difficult.

The real problem was what came next.

“So, why am I here?”

Wasn’t this where the Heavenly Friends Alliance held meetings? Why was he, neither a member of the Alliance nor the Beggars Union, sitting here?

He had come simply because the Alliance leader called him and sat down because he was told to. Then, many others arrived, and this was how things unfolded.

What was even stranger was that none of the gentlemen present found his presence odd.

“Is this really alright?”

Wasn’t this the Heavenly Friends Alliance—the Heavenly Friends Alliance!

Even if it wasn’t the greatest, the Alliance was a major force, counted among the top three powers of Kangho. Was it really acceptable for a meeting here to proceed like this?

“The first thing we need to check is...”

At that moment, Hyun Jong spoke. Gentle Crow Beggar cleared his thoughts and listened carefully.

“It’s about what the Nine Sects along the Yangtze River are doing right now.”

Right.

Gentle Crow Beggar nodded unconsciously in agreement.

Since he had just had friction with the Nine Sects, that was the first thing to check. Certainly, the Mount Hua sect leader was a man with a good grasp of the situation.

“H-has anyone checked it out?”

“...”

“...”

A strange silence fell over the room.

Whenever Hyun Jong looked at them, everyone slightly averted their gaze or claimed with sparkling eyes, “Even if you look that way, we know nothing!”

“... no one?”

“Ahem.”

Tang Gunak coughed, his face red.

“The Alliance leader turned away so strongly that it wasn’t easy to place someone on the riverbank.”

“... true, but...”

Gentle Crow Beggar’s mind spun.

Usually, the Nine Sects’ meetings were formal and dignified, with fierce pride clashing behind the scenes.

Wasn’t the Nine Sects meeting a verbal battle where they hid sharp truths in soft words?

But what should he say about this meeting? It was...

“Should we call it a fistfight or humane?”

No, maybe both. It was so humane that it looked like a fistfight.

“Then, uh... no one knows what the Nine Sects are doing right now?”

“Should I send someone right now?”

“... wouldn’t that be a little awkward?”

“True.”

This was the conversation between Hyun Jong and Tang Gunak, the first and second leaders of the Heavenly Friends Alliance who had shaken the world of Kangho.

“Is this place really alright?”

Hyun Jong, who had ordered the advance on Plum Blossom Island, must have made even Gentle Crow Beggar’s heart beat faster than it had in years.

At that moment, Chung Myung, who had been trampling on Im So-Byeong, suddenly turned his head.

“Why are we talking about that among ourselves? There’s that old beggar over there.”

As soon as he finished, everyone’s eyes turned to Gentle Crow Beggar.

Feeling the sharp, hot gazes, Gentle Crow Beggar coughed repeatedly as if stung.

“But didn’t he come from the Beggars Union?”

When Baek Cheon spoke, Chung Myung snorted.

“From the Beggars Union?”

“That’s what I heard.”

“Then why are the beggar bastards hanging around here before the sun even rose?”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung tilted his neck left and right.

“I thought they came to give us information from the Beggars Union... so maybe they were taking our information?”

Chung Myung smiled brightly and stared at Gentle Crow Beggar.

“Could it be?”

“...”

Gentle Crow Beggar’s body trembled.

‘Mount Hua’s Sword Saint.’

The sect that knew Chung Myung best was naturally Mount Hua. However, the place with the highest notoriety for Chung Myung was not Mount Hua but the Beggars Union.

How many years had passed since the beggars of the world cried over the resentment and sadness expressed by the beggars of Hwa-Um village under Mount Hua?

Cold sweat broke out on Gentle Crow Beggar’s back.

‘What did that Hong Dae-Kwang guy say?’

A devil disguised as a Taoist? The leader of the Evil Sect who had made a mistake choosing his side? If he had joined the Evil Sect, he would have become a legendary figure talked about for five hundred years, but he said he chose the wrong side and was now making flowers with his sword?

“Why aren’t you answering?”

Among all the words, the most vivid in Gentle Crow Beggar’s memory were these:

“That bastard doesn’t respect elders but attacks them. He doesn’t discriminate between men and women. Don’t worry about saving face in front of others and end up with a bloody head. Just do what you have to do.”

At that moment, Gentle Crow Beggar realized what he should say.

“See, young warrior.”

“... Uh?”

He looked at Chung Myung solemnly and spoke.

“I don’t belong now, but I was once an elder of the Beggars Union. Just because I left doesn’t mean my rank vanished. I am still an elder of the Beggars Union, and aren’t you one of the Third Class disciples of Mount Hua?”

Chung Myung twisted his neck left and right.

Crack. Crack.

Then he smiled brightly and asked,

“So?”

“So!”

Gentle Crow Beggar’s eyes glowed.

“I know a lot. Ask me anything! I will tell you everything!”

“...”

Pride was nothing but a luxury for a beggar.

“Currently, the Nine Sects still haven’t retreated from the riverside of the Yangtze.”

“... why?”

“Well, that’s a bit complicated.”

Gentle Crow Beggar scratched his head.

“There are many reasons, but I think two main ones. First, the pirates and the Ten Thousand People clan haven’t retreated yet. The pirates still hold their fleet on the Yangtze River, and the Ten Thousand People clan have disembarked but are camped on the riverbank, ready to head north at any moment.”

“Hmmm.”

Hyun Jong nodded.

“So Shaolin won’t be able to retreat easily. The effect of the Yangtze River treaty hasn’t worn off yet, so they can’t enter the south, but if they back out, the Ten Thousand People clan might take advantage of that gap and head north.”

Hearing this, Chung Myung snickered.

“He is a venomous guy, after all.”

It’s best to treat wounds quickly, whether physical or mental. Untreated wounds fester and leave deep scars.

It seemed Jang Ilso wanted to hold Shaolin’s feet a little longer, letting their wounds fester on the riverbank.

“Second, there’s no reason to retreat.”

“And what reason?”

“Yes, Alliance leader. That is... um, I’m not sure if I should mention this, but the Shaolin abbot...”

“Abbot?”

When Chung Myung’s eyes began to dazzle again, Gentle Crow Beggar quickly changed his words.

“The abbot... man.”

Chung Myung nodded with a pleased expression.

“... Anyway, the abbot summoned the entire Nine Sects before entering the river. However, only a few sects responded.”

“Right. Didn’t he ask us for help too?”

“It means his authority has already fallen as low as it can. But in this situation, if he moved all the way to the Yangtze River and got nothing... no, if he returned to Shaolin without doing anything, what would happen to his pride?”

“... Hmmm.”

Gentle Crow Beggar continued with a bitter expression.

“Shaolin may no longer be able to claim the title of leader of the Nine Sects. A leader can only claim leadership when their name is strong.”

“I understand what you mean.”

Gentle Crow Beggar nodded, looking at Hyun Jong.

“I think the Nine Sects won’t leave this river until the Ten Thousand People clan withdraws. Even more so now that the Peng family has joined them.”

“Hmm.”

Hyun Jong sighed as if saying the battle was over, but the war was not yet finished.

“Thank you, Gul-Gae.”

“No, I heard everyone in the Heavenly Friends Alliance has to earn their keep. So I should earn mine, too.”

Gentle Crow Beggar smiled and fell silent as if his role was over.

Hyun Jong looked back at Tang Gunak.

“Tang family lord.”

“Yes, Alliance Leader.”

“What do we do?”

Tang Gunak stroked his chin thoughtfully before replying,

“Actually, we have no choice right now.”

“Uh? What do you mean?”

“There are too many people in the Namgung family in critical condition. If we move too quickly while they’re barely stabilized, their wounds will worsen and reopen. Even if a great physician comes, we won’t be able to save them.”

“Ah...”

“At least our family needs to treat them here. That will make it difficult for us to move for five days. So for now, I think we should treat the patients and keep watch.”

Hyun Jong nodded.

“Thank you for the advice.”

“You’re welcome.”

Hyun Jong looked at the others and said,

“The purpose of our trip to Plum Blossom Island was to rescue the Namgung family, so let’s stay here until they can move on their own feet.”

“Yes, sect leader.”

“Instead!”

Hyun Jong glared, directing his gaze at only one person.

“You must never leave this manor! Do you understand?”

“Ah, why?”

“Why? Are you asking because you don’t know? The Nine Sects are right in front of us, and if you go outside, will you stay still?”

“Ah, I’m no kid...”

The moment Chung Myung grumbled, everyone who knew him smiled broadly.

“Even a kid is better.”

“Ah, so much better.”

“At least a kid won’t go stabbing a monk in the head.”

“These brats!”

Hyun Jong looked at Chung Myung as if he were inadequate, then turned to Baek Cheon. His eyes softened and became gentler than ever as if they had never been like that before.

“Whoa! Look at the discrimination in your eyes!”

“Shut up, brat!”

Hyun Jong, showing another dramatic change in expression, earnestly urged Baek Cheon,

“Keep a close eye on him so he doesn’t leave the main gate.”

“... You should give me Yu Samae, Baek Sang, Yoon Jong, Jo Gul, and Soso.”

“I’ll give you Un Geom, too.”

“That’s enough.”

“No, I said I won’t go!”

“Shut up!”

Chung Myung pouted.

Hyun Jong glanced at him with a look that said, ‘extremely unattractive,’ and clicked his tongue, straightening his face.

“And then...”

This time, Hyun Jong’s gaze fell on Namgung Do-Wei.

Namgung Do-Wei, who had been watching Chung Myung being bullied with a blank expression, suddenly straightened up.

Finally, the most important words came from Hyun Jong’s mouth.

“We need to discuss the Namgung family’s joining of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

Namgung Do-Wei swallowed hard as tension surged through his body.

Chapter 990: We Saw Nothing. (5)

“Finally!”

Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes, filled with tension, fixed on them.

In truth, there might have been no reason to be nervous. Common sense dictated that no force in the world would dare reject the Namgung family.

It would be a one-in-a-thousand or even one-in-ten-thousand chance, but if the Namgung family declared their intention to join the Heavenly Friends Alliance right then, even Jang Ilso, leader of the Four Precepts Alliance, would defy the persuasion of those around him and cross the Yangtze River.

Namgung Do-Wei glanced back. As expected, Namgung Myung’s face looked relaxed.

“Right. This is normal...”

However, Namgung Do-Wei could not relax at all.

This was the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

Based on what he had seen so far, the common sense of Kangho did not apply to the Heavenly Friends Alliance at all. To put it bluntly, had the Heavenly Friends Alliance ever operated conventionally?

“Phew.”

Namgung Do-Wei let out a deep breath.

He could only trust that sincerity would work. For now, that was the only way.

“Acting Lord.”

Hyun Jong quietly called Namgung Do-Wei. Since he had not yet officially inherited the position of Lord, the title of Acting Lord was appropriate for the situation.

“Yes, Alliance Leader.”

“Let me confirm. Is it true that the Namgung family is requesting to join the Heavenly Friends Alliance?”

“Yes, that is correct.”

Namgung Do-Wei took a deep breath and spoke again.

“I formally request here that we, the Namgung family, request to join the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“Hmm.”

Hyun Jong nodded with a serious expression.

“This may cause problems with your ties to the other sects. Is that acceptable?”

“Yes. Our decision is firm.”

Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes did not waver.

Of course, realistically speaking, the current Namgung family would have no place to stand without the Heavenly Friends Alliance. Emotionally, it was also preferable to be with the Heavenly Friends Alliance rather than the Nine Great Sects.

When both reason and emotion called for the Heavenly Friends Alliance, what was there to hesitate about?

“May I ask the reason?”

“Because I know there is no longer any righteousness with the others.”

Namgung Do-Wei looked at everyone with stern eyes and spoke.

“My late father went to Plum Blossom Island to take responsibility for the Yangtze River massacre incident and to restore the righteous sects’ trust among the people of Kangho. The name of the Blue Sky Namgung family cannot be established without a foundation of righteousness. That was my father’s teaching.”

“Hmmm.”

Tang Gunak closed his eyes tightly. It seemed the mention of Namgung Hwang stirred many thoughts.

“However, the Nine Great Sects prioritized their own interests over righteous actions. That is not wrong. I do not wish to blame them for that, either. But!”

Namgung Do-Wei spoke confidently.

“If my father had not died a violent death and had set foot back on land, he would have definitely wanted to side with the Heavenly Friends Alliance, not the Nine Sects. I believe the future of the Namgung family lies with the Heavenly Friends Alliance, not the Nine Great Sects or the Five Great Families.”

Just as Hyun Jong was about to nod, acknowledging that this reason was sufficient, Namgung Do-Wei spoke again.

“And this isn’t just a decision to overcome the current crisis. I want to instill pride in the children growing up in the Namgung family. We are a sect that pursues righteousness, not merely for cause or profit!”

The disciples of Mount Hua behind him nodded unconsciously.

Actually, pride might seem insignificant. Compared to the enormous gains that come from pursuing profit, what is so great about pride that serves no practical use?

But that useless pride made them straighten their shoulders.

Shaolin could not bear to look the Heavenly Friends Alliance and the Namgung family in the eye because there was no pride in Shaolin, no pride in the Nine Sects.

The disciples of Mount Hua knew that pride did not bring strength. So naturally, they had no choice but to nod.

“So I dare to ask.”

Namgung Do-Wei bowed his head.

“Please accept the Namgung family as a member of the Heavenly Friends Alliance. I will work hard to ensure that the Alliance Leader’s decision is the right one.”

Hyun Jong smiled broadly.

Wonderful.

How many days had it been since Namgung Do-Wei escaped from Plum Blossom Island? No matter how great he was, he could not be free from the shock of losing his father and witnessing his family members die before him.

But Namgung Do-Wei was extremely determined. What he spoke of now was not the past but the future. Having overcome the difficult and painful present, he was moving toward the Namgung family’s future.

How could Hyun Jong not be happy?

Regardless of affiliation, it was natural to feel joy when witnessing such a great ideology. Hyun Jong’s face, which had been hardened since arriving at the Yangtze River, brightened for the first time.

“What do you all think?”

Joy was evident in his voice. Tang Gunak smiled broadly upon hearing Hyun Jong’s happiness.

“The Alliance Leader seems to have already made up his mind, so how can my opinion be of any help?”

“If you say that, I will feel embarrassed.”

At Hyun Jong’s words, Tang Gunak smiled and looked at Namgung Do-Wei.

“There is no reason why it shouldn’t be done. If it is the Namgung family, should we not be the first to bow our heads and ask for their help?”

“Yes.”

“If it is a sect like that, who in the world would refuse? I agree.”

Hyun Jong nodded happily.

“Is there anyone who objects?”

“No one.”

They all answered in unison as if by agreement. None held any particular grudge against the Namgung family, so it was natural. From the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s perspective, accepting the Namgung family and expanding their strength was beneficial.

Even when calculating the pros and cons, this was a huge gain.

Though their power had drastically decreased, they were still the Namgung family. They were not at a level that ordinary sects would dare confront. The title of leader of the Five Great Families was not given without reason.

“This issue is not something that can be decided solely by our opinions, but I don’t think the other sect leaders present will object either. So, with the authority of the Alliance Leader, I will...”

“No!”

“...permit...”

Hyun Jong was about to calmly approve their joining when he flinched and turned his gaze toward the source of opposition.

Im So-Byeong, who usually smiled carefreely while waving his fan, was glaring menacingly at everyone.

“G-Green Forest King...”

“I will not allow it until my eyes are dirtied with dust!”

“Uh...”

“How in the world can this happen! I absolutely cannot sit idly by...”

Bam!

Chung Myung immediately kicked Im So-Byeong away and rolled his eyes.

“Where did this brat get permission to speak? Is this where the Evil Sect nature shows?”

Im So-Byeong was hit in the side and rolled on the floor but did not feel discouraged and raised his head.

“No! Isn’t this really too much? What has the Namgung family done for the Heavenly Friends Alliance? Those brats were just playing around in Anhui, got beaten by the Evil Sects, and now, struggling, they want to come under the shelter of the Heavenly Friends Alliance!”

Im So-Byeong glared fiercely. His force was so intense that not only Hyun Jong but even Tang Gunak flinched.

But unfortunately, there was someone who did not care at all about Im So-Byeong’s force.

Chung Myung, who had been quietly watching Im So-Byeong with wide eyes, poked his eyes with two fingers.

“Ack!”

Im So-Byeong covered his eyes and fell backward. At the same time, everyone unconsciously distorted their faces and covered their eyes.

“Who is this Evil Sect bastard glaring at now? I will gouge your eyes out!”

“E-Euk!”

Im So-Byeong glared at Hyun Jong with eyes full of resentment while wiping tears from his poked eyes. Since it was obvious that talking to Chung Myung was useless, he resolved to find someone else to talk to and cling to.

“No matter how much we are the Evil Sect! We have worked like dogs for the past three years! If you wanted us to carry baggage, we did that! If you wanted us to carry people, we did that! If people wanted us to be escorts, we did that too!”

At Im So-Byeong’s increasingly loud voice, Hyun Jong gradually shrank back.

“We have been exhausted for the past three years! What? Do you want to let the Namgung family join the Heavenly Friends Alliance? And we haven’t even been accepted yet! How can you allow the Namgung family to join while leaving us aside?”

“...”

Hyun Jong had nothing to say, even if given ten mouths.

Actually, thinking about it, for the past three years—no, even before that—Mount Hua had been using Green Forest as much as possible. No ordinary Evil Sect or merchant union would have exploited and taken advantage of a single clan as much as they did.

Of course, all of that was more Chung Myung’s doing than Mount Hua’s, but in any case, if Chung Myung was a member of Mount Hua, then Mount Hua should be responsible.

“People should have a conscience!”

Im So-Byeong snapped. The answer came not from Hyun Jong but from Chung Myung.

“Ah, if you are being treated unfairly, then head over to the Justice faction. You brat! How dare you Evil Sect act like this!”

Im So-Byeong would normally have kept silent at those words, but today, as if he had nothing to lose, he shouted loudly.

“No, I was born with a father who was the King of Green Forest. What do you want me to do? What can I do about that? Oh my, even I want to be reborn! Do you know how it feels to be born with a father who is the leader of an Evil Sect clan?”

“I don’t know. I am an orphan.”

“...Ah, then you definitely can’t understand.”

Im So-Byeong nodded as if he understood.

While everyone else watched the scene as if dumbfounded, Im So-Byeong spoke again.

“I was born into a noble family and wanted to take the governor exam and become a governor of the nation! But my father was a bandit leader. How can I wash my hands of that? I was born like this, so if my father couldn’t give up and I wanted to live a good life, I could only help him! Do you look down on people like this because of their origins?”

“...”

“Abolish the discrimination against the Evil Sect! Such an Alliance Leader is evil...”

Puak!

Chung Myung hit Im So-Byeong, perhaps thinking he had crossed the line.

“No, this brat keeps crossing the line the more I listen to him! Come on, let’s just kill him today!”

“S-Stop it, Chung Myung.”

Perhaps because he thought it was a sight he couldn’t bear to watch without crying, Hyun Jong quickly stopped Chung Myung.

“Ugh, ugh.”

Tang Gunak also covered his mouth with a fist and coughed repeatedly, perhaps feeling embarrassed.

“Actually... among what the Green Forest King said... there was nothing wrong in...”

The disciples of Mount Hua looked at the ceiling and pretended not to notice. They were all embarrassed.

“But that...”

Hyun Jong spoke to Im So-Byeong with a slightly red face.

“I fully understand what the Green Forest King said. And we also know that we now must accept Green Forest into the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“Yes, Sect Leader!”

“But...”

A slightly troubled and awkward expression crossed Hyun Jong’s face.

“Since the Nine Great Sects are trying to accuse us of colluding with the Evil Sect, it isn’t easy to approve your joining right now. So, instead, how about we process it when the situation calms down a bit? I ask for your understanding.”

“Ah, later approval?”

“Yes. That is what I am planning.”

Thinking about it, it made sense.

For the abbot who was trying to link the Heavenly Friends Alliance to the Evil Sect, approving Green Forest’s entry would be a good accusation. Im So-Byeong, who was incredibly smart, couldn’t help but understand this.

But.

He rummaged through his pockets, took out some paper, unfolded it, and immediately handed it to Hyun Jong.

“What is that?”

“A pledge.”

“...A pledge?”

“Yes, it is a pledge to accept Green Forest into the Heavenly Friends Alliance! I will announce it within a year!”

“...”

Hyun Jong looked at the pledge with trembling eyes. The tiny letters were so densely packed they almost hurt his eyes.

“G-Green Forest King?”

“Please sign it.”

“...”

“Right now!”

“...”

Thus, at the place where the Namgung family’s joining was approved, Green Forest’s joining was approved, too.

But it left a subtle sense of uneasiness and discomfort.

Chapter 991: What Kind Of Riches And Honors Will I Enjoy? (1)

“Huhu.”

“...”

“Huhuhu.”

“...”

“Hahaha. Hahahah. Uhahahahahahah!”

“...”

Jo Gul, who had been quietly observing Im So-Byeong’s continuous bursts of laughter, finally spoke softly.

“Sahyung.”

“What?”

“Why is he acting like that?”

“... He must be feeling happy.”

“That much?”

Jo Gul looked at Im So-Byeong with a puzzled expression.

No, he understood it was a good thing... but wasn’t this a bit much?

Yet, Im So-Byeong kept laughing uncontrollably. A man who was usually so sickly and weak that he hardly seemed like the Green Forest King, but seeing him look up at the sky and erupt with laughter made those memories vanish.

Meanwhile...

“Why is he acting like that again?”

“... What?”

“T-that man.”

Yoon Jong’s gaze shifted to the opposite side of the Green Forest King. There, he saw Chung Myung looking gloomier than ever before. So gloomy that one could almost imagine dark clouds gathering above his head.

Im So-Byeong laughed loudly at him, who was crouched in a corner, drawing something in the dirt with a stick. The way he waved the pledge in his hand as if flaunting it, was like a victor waving a flag at a defeated opponent.

“Uhahahahahahaha!”

Yoon Jong shook his head repeatedly as he watched Chung Myung digging into the ground and Im So-Byeong blowing his triumphant horn.

“Such a mess.”

“Right.”

At that moment, Baek Cheon smiled and approached Im So-Byeong.

“Congratulations.”

“Ah! Taoist Baek Cheon! Thank you! Thank you so much!”

“... Do you like it that much?”

Im So-Byeong opened his fan.

“The harsh years, humiliation, and pain I endured just to join the Heavenly Friends Alliance!”

“...”

“How could I not feel good when I’m being compensated for all that at once? Now, our Green Forest is a proud member of the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

“... We decided to announce it later, though...”

“That’s not important. What matters is that we finally have a shield.”

“A shield, you say?”

Im So-Byeong smiled meaningfully at Baek Cheon’s question.

“Think about it, Taoist Baek Cheon. If the Green Forest hadn’t allied with Mount Hua in this situation, what would have happened?”

“Uh? Well...”

Baek Cheon thought for a moment and nodded, understanding Im So-Byeong’s point.

“It would have been dangerous, for sure.”

“Right. Right. Right now, the relationship between the Justice faction and the Evil Sects is at its worst. On top of that, the Nine Great Sects have been humiliated by the Four Precepts Alliance, so they need a way to restore their honor. In this situation, if Green Forest didn’t belong to the Heavenly Friends Alliance, Shaolin—who had been scorned—would turn to us, who have no allies, and destroy us.”

This wasn’t just about Shaolin.

There were more than one or two sects in the Justice faction eager to restore their honor.

“Other sects can simply go down to the Southern lands and join the Four Precepts Alliance, but that’s impossible for Green Forest. Isn’t Green Forest a sect that must survive in the mountains? And most of the mountains in the central plains are concentrated in the north.”

“Right.”

That meant that among the large sects of the central plains, Green Forest was the only one operating in the Southern lands as well, but its influence there was less than a third of its whole force.

“So, Green Forest has two choices: either abandon all the strongholds we’ve nurtured in the Northern lands and move to the Southern lands to bow to the Four Sects Alliance, or cut off the branch in the Southern lands and survive in the Northern lands under the Nine Sects.”

Baek Cheon shook his head.

“The second option would be hard to endure.”

“Yes, that’s right. No matter how strong Green Forest is, it would only be so behind the care of the Nine Sects. If they decide to attack us, it wouldn’t even take them a month to dry up the entire mountain of us.”

“...”

“Any other methods would be the same. How can we bow our heads to Jang Ilso when we’re born human? Especially to that devil!”

“...”

It’s well known that Jang Ilso and Im So-Byeong were enemies. Objectively, it seemed more like Im So-Byeong, who was beaten unilaterally, was nursing a grudge on his own.

“But!”

Clang!

This time, what was spread out was not a fan but a pledge bearing Hyun Jong’s signature.

“Now we are proud members of the Heavenly Friends Alliance! No matter how great the Nine Sects are, they cannot touch us, who are now members of the Heavenly Friends Alliance!”

“...”

Tears suddenly welled up in Im So-Byeong’s eyes as he spoke aloud. Baek Cheon asked in confusion.

“W-why are you crying?”

“No, Taoist Baek Cheon. I suddenly felt sadness...”

“Uh? Sadness?”

“Yes!”

He looked into the distance with wet eyes and said,

“Those harsh times... the devil outside trying to exploit us again, and the idiots inside nagging me about why we had to be servants to the Justice faction...! The elders kept spouting nonsense about how our ancestors wouldn’t be happy with it! That I was not worthy of being the Green Forest King! That Green Forest had become corrupted in its values!”

“... You had such hard times.”

“No, no! All those hardships are over now! I’m so curious to see the expressions on their faces if I shove this pledge in their faces now! Hahahahaha! I will bury them alive where our ancestors are buried. Those damned old farts!”

Baek Cheon wiped the cold sweat and took a couple of steps back.

The reaction was so intense it felt overwhelming to witness, but he liked it...

Meanwhile, Baek Cheon looked at Im So-Byeong again.

‘On the other hand... he is an amazing person.’

That meant Im So-Byeong had a premonition that something like this would happen someday since he first met Chung Myung. At that time, he chose Mount Hua as the sect that could become a shelter for Green Forest.

If Im So-Byeong’s choice had been even a little late or wrong, Green Forest’s situation would have been far more precarious than it is now.

Although he seemed like a strange person, Baek Cheon once again realized that Im So-Byeong had made the best choice as the leader of Green Forest.

On the other hand...

Baek Cheon turned his eyes to the other side. There, Chung Myung, leaning against a tree stump and blankly staring up at the sky, came into view.

He looked so sad that he couldn’t even speak. To the point that Baek Cheon pitied that devil.

“... Chung Myung?”

“...”

“Um... Chung Myung?”

After calling him twice, Chung Myung’s head turned weakly toward Baek Cheon.

“Sasuk...”

“R-right, Chung Myung. Are you okay?”

Baek Cheon swallowed hard.

“I...”

“Uh?”

“... What kind of wealth and honor will I enjoy...”

“...”

Chung Myung’s eyes were wet with tears.

“The day will come when I will be forced to eat from the same pot as Evil Sect bastards. I would rather raise maggots than do that. No matter how much we do it for survival, how could this...”

“C-Calm down.”

“The sect leader is too much! No matter how much his conscience pricks him, he cannot do that! He is accepting the Evil Sect bastards now. And not just one or two, but those rabble-rousing bandits... oh my. Ugh. What is going to happen to Mount Hua now? Oh my, I might be dying. Mount Hua is ruined. Mount Hua is ruined...”

Baek Cheon was so shocked that he broke into a cold sweat.

He had seen Chung Myung curse often enough that it wasn’t new or shocking anymore. But this was the first time he had seen Chung Myung so sad and desperate.

“I should die, I should just die. I should bite down on my tongue and die. What kind of sight will I see if I end up living so long...”

“You’re still young, you insane bastard! What are you talking about like an old man who’s lived all his life!”

Chaos was creeping in.

Jo Gul, watching the scene, asked as if shocked.

“... I guess it must have been quite a shock to be eating from the same pot as the Evil Sect.”

“He is an Evil Sect warrior, too.”

He was already someone who didn’t even treat people like the Evil Sect, but recently, thanks to Jang Ilso’s appearance, his symptoms had worsened several times over.

Even if it was just Im So-Byeong’s case, it would be enough to be called a moderate-level Evil Sect, but if he reacted like that, it’s hard to imagine how much people like Jang Ilso and the Black Dragon King hated him.

“Just to appease him.”

“Can’t we just leave him alone?”
