“I mean, the Beggars Union leader decided to side with the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

They surely provided information to Mount Hua and the Heavenly Friends Alliance. However, that was the only information entrusted to Hong Dae-Kwang. Sensitive information, such as internal facts and trends of the Nine Great Sects, was not relayed to him.

But the fact that Gentle Crow Beggar had decided to move meant the Beggars’ Union was providing sensitive information to the Heavenly Friends Alliance.

That meant...

“... Doesn’t that mean the Beggars Union leader has decided that the Heavenly Friends Alliance will be the force opposing the Nine Sects?”

“There’s no point in finding meaning in it. It already is that way.”

Gentle Crow Beggar shook his head. However, Hong Dae-Kwang couldn’t shake off his worries.

“... To be honest, I’m a bit worried. The Evil Sect is growing stronger day by day, threatening the Northern Lands, and the faction is splitting into two.”

“The world doesn’t always go as planned.”

“But...”

“That isn’t for us to worry about.”

“Uh?”

Gentle Crow Beggar made a slight gesture toward the room where Chung Myung was.

“Doesn’t that man know what we know?”

“...”

“Perhaps Mount Hua’s Sword Saint has been planning this situation for quite some time. So it is his job to discuss countermeasures.”

“Eh. He’s not as planned as you think, elder.”

“Well. What do you think?”

“I am right.”

Gentle Crow Beggar completed Hong Dae-Kwang’s words.

With this incident, the world was clearly divided into three.

South: Four Precepts Alliance; East: Nine Great Sects; and West: Heavenly Friends Alliance.

The boundaries were not yet set. However, the fact that the western part of the Central Plains had been swallowed by the Heavenly Friends Alliance was undeniable.

Gentle Crow Beggar didn’t believe in coincidence. In something like this, human will must be involved.

“It must be hard.”

“Uh?”

“N-no.”

When Hong Dae-Kwang asked, Gentle Crow Beggar shook his head.

But his gaze did not leave the pavilion where Chung Myung was.

Even though it may have taken several years to achieve, or the thought came after much reflection, it’s easy to guess how intense and complicated the mind of the person who achieved all this with his own hands must be.

“Just like a swan may look elegant when seen from above the water, but desperately flaps its legs underwater to stay afloat, Mount Hua’s Sword Saint is also secretly trying to maintain a frivolous appearance.”

“That’s a lie. Swans don’t flap their legs underwater; they simply float.”

“... For real?”

“...”

“...”

Gentle Crow Beggar coughed loudly.

“Anyway, he is an amazing person...”

Clack!

At that moment, the door of Chung Myung’s room swung open.

“Ah. Lately, the alcohol bottles have gotten smaller. I didn’t even take a couple of sips and they’re already empty...”

Chung Myung, walking with both hands full of pure white alcohol bottles, flinched when he saw the two people still standing in front of his room.

“...”

“...”

A strange silence filled the air for a moment. Chung Myung awkwardly held up one of the bottles.

“... Would you like some? There’s a little left.”

“...”

No, maybe it was just a mistake.

Chapter 999: Just Leave It Alone. (4)

“That’s exactly what I thought!”

Namgung Do-Wei gripped Baek Cheon’s hand tightly.

Baek Cheon looked at him with trembling eyes.

“Thank you! Thank you, Taoist Baek Cheon!”

“N-No, wait a minute...”

Baek Cheon, feeling very uneasy, tried to quietly dissuade Namgung Do-Wei, but it seemed he was no longer willing to listen.

“I suspected it, and as expected! I thought Mount Hua’s incredible growth couldn’t be explained by existing techniques.”

“...”

“So it is true that Taoist Baek Cheon and all the members of Mount Hua are learning martial arts from Taoist Chung Myung?”

“... that’s not wrong... I mean, it isn’t wrong, but...”

“Thank you!”

Namgung Do-Wei released Baek Cheon’s hand and clenched his fist.

“It seems the path to revive the Namgung family has been decided now.”

“That... young lord? Please calm down first...”

“Then I will go ask Taoist Chung Myung! Thank you!”

“W-wait a minute...”

But Namgung Do-Wei ran off without even looking back. Baek Cheon, staring blankly after him, was met with criticism.

“Devil.”

“Pushing someone into the fire.”

“Ah, no, kids! What do you want me to do? He didn’t even give me a chance to explain, and only heard what he wanted!”

Baek Cheon grumbled in frustration, but the Five Swords had already turned their gaze away from him, looking pitifully at Namgung Do-Wei running ahead.

“... He will regret this.”

“Yes.”

“Poor guy.”

“That’s just fate, sago.”

The calmest among them was, of course, Yoon Jong.

“... Let’s follow him.”

“Huh?”

Yoon Jong said with a sense of duty on his face.

“If we can’t stop him from jumping into the fire, we should save him when the time is right and pour water on him.”

“...”

Though it seemed oddly out of place, Baek Cheon simply nodded.

“Right, we should try to stop him from burning.”

The Five Swords quickly followed Namgung Do-Wei.

“Ah, don’t push!”

“Wait, I need to see...”

“No, wait, I told you not to push!”

The Five Swords opened the door of the pavilion where Chung Myung was, clinging to the wall and peering inside.

A strange scene unfolded within.

Chung Myung sat like a powerful noble, while Namgung Do-Wei knelt before him.

At first glance, it looked like someone had come to ask for a favor after bribing him.

Like a corrupt official... no, Chung Myung, who looked a hundred times more sinister than any corrupt official, opened his mouth with a sullen expression as he looked at Namgung Do-Wei.

“So...”

“Yes! Taoist.”

“You want me to teach you how to use a sword?”

“Yes!”

Chung Myung smiled broadly.

“Not just you, but the entire Namgung family?”

“Yes!”

“Me?”

“Yes!”

“Haha!”

“Hahahaha!”

“Hahahahaha!”

Chung Myung and Namgung Do-Wei laughed loudly as if sharing a common purpose. Of course, only Namgung Do-Wei thought he understood the meaning.

“This bastard must have gone crazy!”

Puak!

“Acck!”

Namgung Do-Wei screamed as he flew backward after being kicked in the face by Chung Myung, who had jumped over the table.

“I knew this would happen!”

Baek Cheon burst through the door and quickly caught Namgung Do-Wei before he slammed into the wall.

“No!”

Chung Myung roared, stomping the floor.

“Who put drugs into the river without telling me!? There’s not a single sane brat left! The Green Forest bastard wants to join the Heavenly Friends Alliance! The Shaolin brat only cares about himself! And what about the brat who wants the Namgung family to learn swordsmanship after becoming the lord?”

“T-taoist, it’s not like that...”

“I’ve seen many bastards in my lifetime, but this one is on another level of stupidity! Yah! You insane bastard! Does any of that make sense?”

Baek Cheon, who had set down the gloomy Namgung Do-Wei, covered his hand with a fist and coughed softly.

“That... Chung Myung.”

“What!”

“I understand why you’re upset, but even the young lord is so anxious he’s acting like this. You have to understand...”

“Sasuk...”

“Uh?”

Chung Myung said with a serious expression.

“If we continue like this, we’ll all go down in flames.”

“Why do people always talk about going down in flames...”

“Anyway, we’re doomed. We’re going down in flames. The only ones left behind will be the kids.”

“... So, what’s wrong?”

“But if Jo Gul sahyung, who survived, goes to Wudang with the others left behind and begs them to teach fighting, how would the sasuk who passed away feel about it?”

“This insane bastard!”

“Kuak!”

Baek Cheon kicked Jo Gul, who was standing there clueless. Jo Gul doubled over in pain and fell.

“Ah, I was a step too late...”

Yoon Jong licked his lips and lowered his raised hand. This time, Baek Cheon was quicker.

“Noooo!”

Jo Gul, rolling on the floor, raised his head with wide eyes.

“Why are you hitting me? Why! I didn’t really do it! So why?

“We’re doing this because we think you would actually do it.”

“...”

Jo Gul, speechless, turned to Yoon Jong, who simply nodded with a faint smile.

Betrayed by the world, Jo Gul crouched down, wrapping his arms around his knees. Tang Soso, nearby, patted him on the shoulder, feeling sorry for him.

“Right, you should have acted like a human from the start.”

“Kuak! Will you not leave right now?”

Baek Cheon coughed in vain and glanced at Namgung Do-Wei.

‘This isn’t right.’

He hadn’t thought much of it before, but changing his perspective, he realized there was no one like that insane guy. Of course, that was the truth. Where else would one learn sword technique?

Even Hyun Jong, famous for not showing pride in Mount Hua, didn’t ask the Southern Edge sect to teach swordsmanship, even though Mount Hua was in a terrible situation then.

No, if a disciple had suggested such a thing, even Hyun Jong would have stuck a plum branch in their face.

Chung Myung shouted, enraged.

“Yah, you insane bastard. Where did you learn the sword? Namgung Hwang in the tomb will rise up, coughing out Yangtze river water!”

“... My father isn’t buried yet.”

“Then go confirm it, brat! He might be trying to break through the coffin right now!”

Namgung Do-Wei flinched at Chung Myung’s force.

Chung Myung clicked his tongue and shook his head in disbelief.

“The world is about to fall. It must be falling now. What the hell happened in the past 100 years that the world is now filled with idiots like these?”

“Now these brats are scarier than the Heavenly Demon. Scarier than the Heavenly Demon, you morons!”

But Namgung Do-Wei protested as if it were unfair.

“I-I am not asking you to teach me Mount Hua’s sword techniques, Taoist.”

“Uh?”

“No matter how insane I may be, how could I ask you to teach me Mount Hua’s sword technique?”

“Right?”

Chung Myung, suddenly calm, nodded with a face that said, ‘Well, that’s right.’

“That’s good, then. For the future of the Namgung family, I was thinking whether I should throw you into the Yangtze River and make Namgung Myung the head of the family.”

“...”

“Then what?”

“Of course, I will learn the Namgung family’s sword technique. Namgung isn’t weak enough to covet other sects’ techniques!”

“It’s a little weak, though.”

“...”

“Uh... no, well. Anyway, continue.”

“... So, the thing is...”

Namgung Do-Wei, feeling somewhat sad, wiped his eyes and continued.

“What we want from you, Taoist, is not sword technique teachings, but experience in real combat.”

“Uh?”

“I felt it deeply during Plum Blossom Island. Learning sword technique without error, operating it perfectly, and identifying weaknesses through sparring with each other...”

Namgung Do-Wei’s attitude gradually became serious.

“That alone is not enough for real combat.”

“...”

Chung Myung looked at Namgung Do-Wei with interest.

‘He’s an interesting guy.’

In this incident, he lost two-thirds of the family sect’s power and even the lordship of the family. Because of that, the time for him to inherit the lord position came at least 20 years earlier.

An ordinary person would be out of their mind in such a situation. However, Namgung Do-Wei was already looking ahead.

“It might not be something to say in such a situation, but the Namgung family’s sword is by no means dull.”

Chung Myung nodded slightly. It wasn’t wrong. If the Namgung family had been weak, they wouldn’t have maintained their position as the best family sect in the world for so long.

“Even so, the reason the Namgung family is in such a great crisis this time is because our sword was like a sword in a well, unaware of how to fight in the real world.”

Namgung Do-Wei stared straight at Chung Myung.

“However, Mount Hua was different. The Mount Hua sect members probably didn’t have much fighting experience, but they fought as if they’d been through countless battles.”

Namgung Do-Wei clenched his fists.

“Taoist.”

“...”

“Please help us. We absolutely must become stronger now. And I think the only person who can do that job is a Taoist.”

“Hmm.”

Chung Myung scratched his cheek with an odd look, then spoke to Namgung Do-Wei in a slightly different tone than before.

“Well, everything is right, but... let me ask you one thing.”

“Yes.”

“I understand why, but I think that’s something Namgung could do without asking to learn it from Mount Hua.”

“...”

“I’m saying this because I don’t think you don’t know how dangerous that is. People in the world won’t consider such circumstances. They’ll just say the Namgung family is insane for learning swordsmanship from Mount Hua.”

“... I know.”

“Yeah, you know. I think you know. That’s why I’m asking.”

Chung Myung said coldly.

“If you know all that, why are you doing this?”

“...”

Namgung Do-Wei bit his lips tightly.

“... No more...”

“Hmm?”

“Because I don’t want to lose anyone anymore.”

Chung Myung’s face hardened at those words.

“The existence of the Four Precepts Alliance will make the world more and more dangerous. Unless you choose to close the Gate... no, even if you do, the Namgung family can no longer avoid them.”

The Five Swords listened and nodded. There was certainly truth in those words.

“Father told me.”

“... Namgung Hwang? The lord?”

“Yes. Father showed me at the last moment what the lord of the Namgung family should do.”

Namgung Do-Wei said with firm eyes.

“The lord of the Namgung family is the one who protects the Namgung family.”

“...”

“I... I don’t want to lose a single family member anymore. I will do anything for that.”

Namgung Do-Wei raised his head and looked at Chung Myung.

“If you tell me to kneel, I will kneel. If you tell me to bow, I will bow.”

Then he bowed deeply and shouted.

“Please help me, Taoist. That debt will be repaid by the Namgung sword!”

Baek Cheon, who had been watching, nodded.

‘That’s amazing.’

It was enough to make him feel ashamed.

‘If it were me, would I be able to bow my head to someone else like that for the sake of Mount Hua’s future?’

Namgung Do-Wei lived in a much higher status than Baek Cheon. Wasn’t the position of eldest son of the Namgung family one everyone in the murim world aspired to?

But Namgung Do-Wei’s actions now showed no hesitation. Regardless of right or wrong, that attitude was definitely worth emulating.

“I won’t lose a single person...”

Chung Myung snickered as he briefly recalled Namgung Do-Wei’s words.

“You can say such dreamy words even after seeing hell?”

“... I think I should say it even more because I saw it.”

“Your mouth is still alive, brat.”

A smile formed at the corners of Chung Myung’s mouth.

However, Baek Cheon knew the smile wasn’t meant badly. He sometimes smiled like that when they did more than Chung Myung expected.

“Well, fine. Even if it’s a real fight, it’s not that hard since you just have to beat the people up.”

“T-then?”

“We’re already an alliance. I will help you.”

“T-taoist!”

Namgung Do-Wei looked at Chung Myung with a moved face and shining eyes.

“Thank you...”

He was about to bow deeply again, but flinched and stopped. Chung Myung’s palm was waiting where he was about to bow.

“T-taoist.”

Namgung Do-Wei raised his head, puzzled.

And he saw a delighted Chung Myung, literally overjoyed.

After waiting and fighting for so long, when Jiang Taigong finally caught a moonfish, this must be the face he had.

“So.”

“...”

“How much are you going to pay?”

“... Uh?”

Namgung Do-Wei’s eyes widened.

“You said you don’t want to lose even one person.”

“...”

“If you say it the other way around, that means each and every life is that expensive, right?”

“... Taoist?”

“Then let’s find out.”

Chung Myung let out a wicked laugh.

“How much is the Namgung family willing to pay to save those people?”

“...”

“Don’t disappoint me. The Namgung family is rich, right?”

“...”

“Ehehehehe! Ehehehehehe!”

As Chung Myung laughed, a pure white marten suddenly jumped from his arms and climbed onto his head, mimicking the same pose and laughter.

Only after seeing this bizarre sight did Namgung Do-Wei realize something was seriously wrong.

Chapter 1000: Just Leave It Alone. (5)

“Why is the list of assets suddenly...?”

“...”

“Ah, of course. Since the young lord is now the head of the family, you have the right to inspect the family’s assets. But why now...?”

Namgung Myung looked at Namgung Do-Wei with suspicion. Namgung Do-Wei mumbled with a hollow expression.

“Just...”

“Uh?”

“Don’t ask... just answer. Just...”

“...”

What on earth had happened to the man who left the house this morning full of confidence, only to return with eyes like someone who had lost everything gambling?

Namgung Myung, who had been eyeing Namgung Do-Wei warily, said nervously,

“Then I will hand you the organized ledger.”

“Uncle.”

“...Why do you keep doing that?”

“Our Namgung family is surely rich, right?”

“Hehe.”

Namgung Myung laughed as if it were absurd.

“What are you talking about? They say there are many merchants in the world, and many people so wealthy they don’t need to work. But no matter how great the wealth, it can’t compare to the Namgung family’s fortune.”

His statement was full of pride.

Of course, as far as Namgung Myung knew, the only sect that could rival the Namgung family’s wealth was Shaolin. Even Shaolin or the Peng family were, in reality, inferior to the Namgung family.

However, upon hearing this, Namgung Do-Wei didn’t look pleased; instead, he began biting his nails nervously.

“We are rich... we are rich... rich, huh.”

“Y-young lord?”

“So we... are rich...”

Was this guy insane?

Clench.

Namgung Do-Wei, who had been biting his fingernails, finally opened his trembling lips.

“Where can I get the ledger?”

“If it’s a copy, the finance leader should have it.”

“...Right.”

Watching Namgung Do-Wei walk away, Namgung Myung was overcome with an inexplicable sense of anxiety.

He could vaguely hear Namgung Do-Wei mumbling, “We’re doing well, we’re doing well. We have to do well, please let us do well.”

“What the hell...”

Namgung Myung still didn’t know the exact nature of the disaster approaching the Namgung family.

Chung Myung’s eyes grew as wide as lanterns.

His mouth fell open involuntarily, and his hands holding the ledger trembled as if chilled.

“...T-this is...”

“...”

“Is this all?”

Namgung Do-Wei nodded his head, trembling.

“This is the list of assets of the Namgung family.”

“...O-oh my god.”

Chung Myung’s head creaked as he turned on its own. Even if the Heavenly Demon appeared before him, he wouldn’t have been so shocked.

He kept looking back and forth between Namgung Do-Wei and the ledger, speaking his thoughts without holding back.

“I wondered why people starve despite working hard, but you guys had all the money. You thieves.”

“...”

“Uh... what is this... hehehe.”

Chung Myung looked at the ledger again with a face that said he was fed up.

To be clear, Mount Hua was no longer a sect without money. It might not have been so a few years ago, but now it could be called one of the richest sects in the world.

Their accumulated wealth might not rival the richest in the Central Plains, but considering their income, they could now outmatch most sects.

In terms of income alone, they could boast of surpassing Mount Hua’s past poverty. But...

“This... damn. They say those who really have money have more than we.”

Baek Cheon slowly approached, curious about Chung Myung’s intense reaction.

“How much money do they even have?”

“See.”

Chung Myung handed the ledger to Baek Cheon without explanation.

Baek Cheon flipped through a few pages and casually said,

“No, it’s possible to have lots of money. It’s the Namgung family’s world. But it’s a bit much to say to the young lord...”

At that moment, Yoon Jong witnessed a sight he had never seen before.

Baek Cheon’s eyes slowly widened until they nearly popped.

“T-this...”

Flutter flutter!

Baek Cheon’s hands, turning over the ledger, began moving violently.

He had been roughly flipping through the pages with an expression that didn’t match his neat face, but suddenly stopped. Then he raised his head and blankly looked at Namgung Do-Wei.

But that was only for a moment, as his blank expression quickly shifted into one of resentment. Namgung Do-Wei mumbled.

“W-what is it?”

“You rich brat...”

“Uh?”

Namgung Do-Wei was so shocked he doubted his ears. Did those words really come from Baek Cheon?

At that moment, Chung Myung shouted,

“You have so much money, why bother wielding a sword, you insane bastard!”

“W-what is that even supposed to mean...”

“The rich get richer! They’re really getting richer!”

A voice full of resentment flowed from Chung Myung’s mouth.

“Damn it. There’s a limit to how unfair the world can be! Some people are born orphans and barely survive in Mount Hua, which is worse than a beggar’s den! Some are born as sons of the Namgung family!”

“I know!”

“Sasuk is from Southern Edge!”

“Compared to Namgung, Southern Edge is also a beggar’s den!”

“Ah, that’s right.”

“Filthy rich bastards.”

“Damned landowners.”

Jo Gul rubbed his eyes as he watched the scene. Somehow, it seemed like a red aura was surging behind Chung Myung and Baek Cheon. He thought he could see a sickle and hammer...

“Oh my... I thought I had a lot of money until now.”

Chung Myung mumbled with a blank expression.

Now he could see clearly. That damned Chung Mun sahyung’s brainwashing was narrowing his world. No matter how much Chung Myung boasted about making a lot of money, in the end, he was just a Taoist fool, happy with food and drink.

“Wait. So the Shaolin and Wudang brats have about the same amount of money, then?”

Chung Myung clenched his teeth.

“But these brats still want funds to raise their sects? I should chase these guys and dig up their bloody graves, too.”

“...What are you talking about? Since when?”

“Kuak.”

Chung Myung scratched his neck as if he had swallowed hot charcoal.

Yu Yiseol, curious about their foaming-at-the-mouth reaction, quietly approached Chung Myung and snatched the ledger.

Then.

Thud.

“Kuak! Sago! Why are you collapsing?”

Tang Soso ran to Yu Yiseol, who had fallen back in shock.

Chung Myung clicked his tongue as he watched.

“Right, right. It’s normal for someone from the streets to faint when they see this much money.”

“Sago is no orphan!”

“Something similar.”

“No, how much money does he have to make such a mess of us?”

Tang Soso picked up the ledger and looked at it.

“Hmm.”

Then, with a slightly indifferent expression, she handed it over to Jo Gul. Jo Gul also read it briefly and said,

“...It’s just an ordinary ledger, right?”

“If it’s the Namgung family, shouldn’t this much be basic?”

Baek Cheon and Chung Myung’s eyes turned red at their reactions. Yu Yiseol, lying on the ground, raised her head and grabbed the sword at her waist.

“Dirty lords of the land.”

“Children of the rich!”

“They need to die!”

The trio of orphans, runaways, and street children glared at the three born with silver spoons in their mouths.

“Now, wait a minute! I don’t know about others, but Sasuk is a bit different!”

“Right! Sasuk is from a good family too! Those people are like that because they came from poverty!”

Jo Gul was confident this attack would work. However, the moment Baek Cheon answered, Jo Gul gave up on his counterattack.

“When I was born, my brother was Jin Geum-Ryong.”

“Woah...”

“This is the truth.”

“Stay there, Sasuk. Excuse me for this.”

In an atmosphere where the flames of revolution seemed about to flare up, Yoon Jong stepped in swiftly to mediate.

“T-this isn’t the time to fight among ourselves, Chung Myung.”

“Kuaak.”

Chung Myung twisted his neck.

“Euk. Euk.”

His stomach churned, but didn’t this ledger fall into their hands anyway?

“That...”

Chung Myung looked back and forth between the ledger and Namgung Do-Wei, then moved his lips with difficulty.

“...I can do it.”

“Uh?”

“...I-I... can... ah, no. This...”

At that moment, Chung Myung realized something.

Deep inside him, there clearly existed a conscience he hadn’t even known.

Tears flowed down Chung Myung’s cheeks.

‘I cannot ask for more...’

Of course, his labor was so valuable that it couldn’t be exchanged for gold. The problem was that the Namgung family’s wealth was so vast that gold was like a foot rag to them.

No matter how highly Chung Myung thought of himself, he couldn’t ask for too much money in exchange for teaching a few swordsmen.

‘Sahyung! Chung Mun sahyung! Today, I finally realized what conscience is, sahyung!’

–Is that conscience? You insane bastard!

Ah, just stay quiet for now!

Chung Myung took a deep breath and opened his mouth.

“If... you... Give me the ledger, how can...”

“Uh? This ledger?”

When Namgung Do-Wei asked in a puzzled tone, Chung Myung shrugged and spoke in a hesitant voice.

“T-then I will...”

Later, Jo Gul recalled this scene as:

It was like the sun in the middle of the sky shrinking and the water in Dongting Lake drying up—the Chung Myung of this world being oppressed by nothing but money.

At that moment, Namgung Do-Wei shook his head.

“It isn’t that, Taoist. The money is too little.”

“...Uh?”

“Namgung’s life was saved. And now we are part of the Heavenly Friends Alliance, right?”

“R-right...?”

“I will give you five tenths of the property. Please use it as training funds for the Heavenly Friends Alliance.”

Thud.

The ledger fell from Chung Myung’s hand.

With a face like he had seen a ghost, Chung Myung’s chin trembled.

“F-five... five? F-five...?”

“Yes!”

Namgung Do-Wei nodded firmly.

“I think it would be fine if they divided it appropriately: Taoists’ share, Mount Hua’s share, and the Heavenly Friends Alliance’s share.”

“Are you really giving me five tenths...? Of this?”

“Of course.”

Namgung Do-Wei lightly patted his chest.

“No matter how important that property is, it cannot compare to the grace of having our lives saved. Please understand that we can only repay the favor like this.”

Chung Myung suddenly ran to him and grabbed his hand.

“T-thank you! Thank you!”

“T-taoist! Why are you doing this? It’s nothing.”

Tears streamed down Chung Myung’s cheeks.

‘It’s nothing to you.’

Thank goodness.

It was truly fortunate that the guy was a moron.

Uh? This wasn’t being a moron, but being kind? That’s also being a moron.

“I...”

“Uh?”

“I will give it my all! I will do whatever it takes to make the Namgung family act like a real sect! I will do my best!”

“....”

“Absolutely!”

“Thank you.”

At that moment, Jo Gul, who was watching quietly, whispered to Yoon Jong.

“Sahyung. The situation is a bit odd, but if you think about it, isn’t this like getting hit for money?”

“Shh. Be quiet now.”

“...”

That day, the Namgung family became Mount Hua’s friends.

A true friend whose friendship came from the heart...
